OTHER AGENT (CLAUDE) SAID:
# Candle ingestion review, round 3: implemented vs. pending architecture

The verdict stays **REJECTED**. This round changes three things:

- **OBSERVE is worse than both reviews said.** Started in the main trader, it runs the trader's own WebSocket and trades from it, without any of the safeguards that only exist in OFF mode.
- **Restart finding A was overstated**, and I've narrowed it. In practice it still hits the 1m candle on every deploy.
- **The duplicate `local-candle:` key also affects deployed starts.** The deploy script keeps the bundled `application.yml`.

Nothing was modified. I couldn't write a plan file or call ExitPlanMode because neither tool is available in this session.

# FINDINGS

## Corrections to my previous report

**1. Finding A ("every restart") was overstated.**
- It happens only when the downtime runs past the close time of a candle that was still open at shutdown.
- For 1m candles that is practically every deploy. The deploy script drains the old JVM (up to 120 s) and then starts a new one before the bootstrap runs (`Deploy-Trader.ps1:111-135`). 5m and 1h candles are affected only when one of their closes falls inside the downtime.
- The defect itself is confirmed:
  - `MarketDataService.importCandles` keeps every Binance candle whose close time has passed (`:45`) and sends it to the `BACKFILL` update clause (`CandleRepository.java:173-188`).
  - That clause closes the existing `RUNTIME` row without a token, leaves its provenance as `RUNTIME`, and writes no outcome.
  - The detector needs a token (`LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:50`), so it never picks the row up.
- There is a broader version that I'm flagging but not counting as a confirmed violation. Every candle missed during downtime is inserted as `BACKFILL` with no audit row and no outcome. Gap reconciliation then skips it because the row is present (`LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:150-155`). Whether AGENTS.md's "one durable terminal outcome" rule covers bootstrap history is a judgement call.

**2. Finding B was too narrow, and its unknown is now partly resolved.**
- The CandleClosed event path has no mode check. `grep` across `indicator/event` finds `mode` only in the worker's latency logging (`CandleClosedAnalysisWorker.java:216-218`).
- So in OBSERVE, a main-trader WebSocket close is analysed and traded exactly as in OFF.
- Every FIX-153 safeguard returns early outside OFF:
  - instance lock (`LocalTraderInstance.java:19`)
  - schema checks (`LocalRuntimeSchemaChecks.java:17`)
  - detector (`LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:36`)
  - reconciliation (`LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:93`)
  - exchange clock (`ExchangeClockSynchronizer.java:39`)
  - price-event resolver (`MarketPriceEventService.java:104`)
- Price events are still written as `PENDING` (`:62`), and nothing resolves them in OBSERVE. A later Replay over that window then fails (`assertReplayEvidence`, `:152-154`).
- The freshness gate does not filter by where a signal came from. It only checks that the signal's candle is the newest closed one and is still within its grace period (`SignalProcessingFreshness.java:31-38`, 90 s for 1m). The 1m STARTUP signal from the bootstrap is built on the newest closed candle, so it will normally pass.
- **How reachable this is:** OBSERVE was built for the separate approval console, which lives outside the main component scan (`CutoverApprovalApplication.java:8-18`). The deploy script forces `--shared-market.mode=OFF` (`Deploy-Trader.ps1:127`). So the main trader runs OBSERVE only through `SHARED_MARKET_MODE=OBSERVE` or a manual/IDE start, and nothing in the trader rejects that. This is a latent hazard, not the default path.

**3. Finding C now has a deployment angle, but Spring Boot's behaviour is still unverified.**
- The duplicate key is confirmed (`application.yml:40` and `:447`), and nothing else in the repo declares it.
- The deploy keeps "external application.yml resolution" and adds command-line overrides (`Deploy-Trader.ps1:124`), so the bundled `application.yml` still loads on deployed starts.
- The only evidence I have that Boot rejects duplicate keys is my recollection of how its YAML loader works. I haven't checked that for Boot 4.1.0.
- No log in the repo shows a start with this file: I searched `logs/crypto-ai-trader.log` for startup and duplicate-key lines and found nothing. No test loads the configuration.

## Challenges to Codex

- **Codex: OBSERVE "does not execute collector-driven protection/analysis" and "is not double trading execution."** Both are correct for the collector consumer (`SharedMarketConsumer.java:479-481`). Codex missed that the trader's own path executes trades in OBSERVE without the OFF safeguards. That is the real risk, not the collector link.
- **Codex: the ordinary REST import can close a live row in "the reachable overlap" during coin activation, or through the import API.** True, but Codex missed the deterministic case: the startup bootstrap closes the row left open at shutdown (correction 1).
- **Codex: no visible shared rate-limit policy.** Confirmed earlier: nothing in `src/main/java` handles HTTP 429/418, `Retry-After` or `X-MBX` headers. Note that bootstrap makes one server-time call plus one klines call per symbol and interval (4 × 3 by default) in a burst, with no shared budget.
- **Codex: Replay doesn't match production's candle selection; reconciliation can leave `PENDING` rows that never finish and can be crowded out by `LIMIT 50`.** Agreed; re-checked at `LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:129-140, 194-196` and `LocalReconciliationAuditRepository.java:35-38`.
- **Codex's evidence citations are accurate.** I found no fabricated line references.

## Confirmed in code (unchanged)

- **Who owns ingestion:**
  - **OFF:** the trader owns the WebSocket, REST import and writes to `crypto_ai.candle`, and reads its own database (`SharedMarketSource.java:23`).
  - **LIVE:** candle reads go to the collector database, and local writes and direct ingestion are refused (`:45, :54-56`; `BinanceKlineService.java:56`).
  - **OBSERVE:** reads stay local because `enabled` is true only in LIVE (`:22, :45`), but the collector connection pool is still created and its schema checked (`:24-42`).
- **Close handling:** a token is set only when a row goes from open to closed (`CandleRepository.java:201`), and only the write that owns that token dispatches analysis (`:120`, `BinanceKlineService.java:110-111`).
- **Recovery code** matches the pending design but is switched off (`application.yml:44`), and the deploy script doesn't switch it on.

5. **The detector keeps its retry state only in memory** and only looks back 180–600 s. After a longer outage, candles get no outcome.
6. **No shared Binance REST rate-limit or ban handling.**
7. **Replay parity with production's candle selection is not shown.**
8. **Trader-only fresh install via B105 is unproven,** and V103 still needs `crypto_ai_v2`.

# VERDICT

YOUR PRIOR FINDINGS:
## FINDINGS

The checked-in implementation substantially realizes the pending local-candle direction in source, but it does not safely meet that direction’s release requirements. The appropriate verdict is rejection of the architecture as ready/consistent, not authorization for any change.

1. **Kline WebSocket ownership**
   - In `OFF` and `OBSERVE`, the trader starts its own Binance WebSocket and processes klines.
   - In `LIVE`, trader WebSocket ingestion is disabled; the retained collector path is used instead.

2. **REST/bootstrap ownership**
   - In `OFF` and `OBSERVE`, the trader calls Binance REST for server time and klines, then writes local candles.
   - In `LIVE`, imports/bootstrap are refused.

3. **Persistence and collector coupling**
   - `OFF`: candle reads/writes use local `crypto_ai.candle`.
   - `LIVE`: candle reads route to the collector datasource/schema (`crypto_ai_v2`); local writing is rejected.
   - `OBSERVE`: local candle reads/writes remain active, while the shared consumer separately reads collector stream/cursor tables. Thus OBSERVE combines direct ingestion with collector connectivity.

4. **Close transition**
   - The runtime WebSocket path is correctly structured: only an atomic open→closed runtime write assigns a close token; only the receipt owning that token publishes close analysis. Processing order is input → protection → observer → close dispatch; analysis is after commit and executor-backed.

5. **Material backfill defect**
   - Ordinary REST import uses a duplicate-key update that may close an existing `closed=0` runtime candle. It does not assign a runtime close token, write an analysis outcome, or dispatch analysis.
   - This is not just a narrow live race. On checked-in defaults, bootstrap runs before the WebSocket starts. A candle left open at shutdown can be closed by startup REST import and become permanently ineligible for both normal close dispatch and reconciliation.
   - The effect depends on bootstrap being enabled, the relevant candle being included in the REST window, and the process having persisted an open row before shutdown. Those conditions are the checked-in normal path, but “every restart” is too absolute.

6. **Reconciliation**
   - A dedicated reconciliation implementation exists and uses the right primitives for its own path: update-only recovery of an existing open row, insert-if-absent gap repair, no close token, and `SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE` outcome.
   - It is disabled in checked-in configuration.
   - Its retry/audit design can leave `PENDING` rows indefinitely after they leave the active window, and candidate SQL applies `LIMIT 50` before in-memory due/loss-evidence filtering.

7. **OBSERVE contradiction**
   - OBSERVE is not execution-equivalent to LIVE shared processing: collector deliveries are marked `OBSERVED`.
   - However, OBSERVE bootstrap explicitly calculates an indicator, creates a `STARTUP` signal, and invokes paper-trading processing after REST import. It also runs the collector consumer. This contradicts the requested separation and is more serious than merely an observational collector connection.

8. **Replay**
   - Fresh Replay selects closed candles by closure/time predicates, not by runtime provenance, close token, reconciliation outcome, or `SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE`.
   - Therefore the source does not establish Replay parity with production’s local live-admission boundary.

9. **Rate limits**
   - I found no shared Binance REST policy handling 429/418, `Retry-After`, Binance usage headers, or a shared budget spanning bootstrap, reconciliation, and exchange-clock calls.

10. **Bootstrap**
   - `V103` explicitly requires `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`. `B105` claims a trader-only baseline effect, but source review cannot prove the Flyway/MySQL behavior. The fresh trader-only bootstrap gate remains unresolved.

11. **Documentation contradictions**
   - `AGENTS.md` calls trader-owned ingestion a pending design decision, while source and both architecture documents describe FIX-153 as implemented in source but unapproved.
   - `claude-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` incorrectly says direct ingestion entry points are disabled in both LIVE and OBSERVE. Code disables them only in LIVE.
   - `codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` correctly identifies the unsafe ordinary REST finalization, yet calls automatic reconciliation “pending” immediately before acknowledging a first reconciliation implementation slice. It does not distinguish the implemented-but-disabled slice from the still-unmet release design clearly enough.
   - The `CandleRepository` comment says ordinary backfill “inserts missing identities only,” directly contradicting its duplicate-key update SQL.

## Trade investigations
When asked to investigate a trade:

- Confirm the application, environment, symbol, and trade ID.
- Use configured database and SSH connections without displaying secrets.
- Use read-only, bounded queries and targeted log searches.
- Read the actual schema before choosing tables or columns.
- Reconstruct the timeline: candle, signal, decision, entry, protection,
  and exit. Display timestamps in KSA (UTC+03:00), interpreting stored
  timestamps as UTC.
- Trace the relevant code and configuration for each decision.
- Check data freshness, entry quality, strategy, risk/reward, fees,
  stop-loss, and exit reason where applicable.
- Assess the entry using only information available at that moment.
  Use subsequent price action separately to evaluate the outcome.
- Distinguish a valid losing trade from a logic defect, stale data,
  execution delay, configuration issue, or missing evidence.
- Check whether the behavior can be reproduced in Replay.
- Support findings with record IDs, log timestamps, and code references.
- State uncertainties; do not invent a cause.
- Do not change code, configuration, services, or production data during
  an investigation unless the user requests a fix.

Report:
1. What happened.
2. Why the system entered and exited.
3. Whether the behavior followed the intended rules.
4. Confirmed defects and missing evidence.
5. Recommended correction and regression checks, if needed.

## Current architecture direction (pending implementation)

The current priority is to remove the trader's runtime dependency on the
collector. This is a design decision, not an implemented/deployed change.

- Target runtime: trader-owned Binance kline ingestion and local
  `crypto_ai.candle` writes with `shared-market.mode=OFF`.
- The collector remains independent in `crypto_ai_v2`; the trader must not
  connect to, read from, or write collector stream/cursor tables at runtime.
- Do not run direct ingestion and the shared-market executors together.
- Applied shared-market tables and Flyway history are historical evidence; do
  not drop, reset, or edit them merely to enable the new path.
- `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` still requires
  `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event` for a fresh Flyway install. Treat
  trader-only bootstrap as a release blocker until a forward, tested installer
  policy exists. Never edit an applied Flyway migration.

## Local-candle implementation safety

When implementing or reviewing the local-candle design:

- Keep `shared-market.fix142.enabled=false`,
  `shared-market.fix142.acknowledgment-enabled=false`, and scheduled analysis
  disabled for the OFF-mode cutover unless a reviewed change explicitly says
  otherwise.
- Preserve the input -> protection -> observer -> close-dispatch transaction
  order. Candle close analysis must not run on the Binance ingestion thread.
- A closed candle needs durable/recoverable analysis admission; an in-memory
  event/queue alone loses work across a crash. Recovery must distinguish fresh
  execution-capable work from historical-only analysis.
- Candle updates must be monotonic: a late open update cannot reopen or
  overwrite a closed candle. Publish close work only for an atomic
  open-to-closed transition.
- A WebSocket reconnect can occur at a candle-close boundary and Binance may
  not replay that final closed-kline event. Candle recovery is automatic: use
  an exact authoritative Binance REST candle only after exchange-time
  finality and the measured live-close grace. It may either finalise a matching
  existing `closed=0` row or insert a wholly absent identity inside the active
  data-quality window. A recovered open row preserves provenance; an inserted
  gap row uses `BACKFILL` provenance. Neither path may alter a completed row,
  create a close-transition token, dispatch analysis, create a live signal,
  or execute a trade. The recovery must have durable retry/audit evidence,
  bounded rate-limit handling, health visibility, and no manual repair step.
- Automation direction: no pending candle or analysis outcome may require
  manual approval, manual database repair, or a persisted trading fence.
  Reconciliation must retry and resolve automatically, or age out to a durable
  explicit outcome with a visible reason. This does not permit unverified data
  to masquerade as valid or force an entry: a genuine strategy/risk veto or
  unavailable authoritative data remains non-executable evidence, not a
  silently stuck pending signal.
- Gap backfill must be a pure insert-if-absent operation. It must never use a
  duplicate-key update that could finalise or alter a live row which appeared
  while the authoritative REST request was in progress. Recovery candidates
  are bounded by the active quality window and terminal audit outcomes are not
  retried. Each candle/work path must write one durable terminal analysis
  outcome; transient failures remain retryable and write no terminal outcome.
- Any future universal trading-mutation barrier is an in-memory drain
  participant only. Outside an active drain it must not block, delay, queue,
  or persistently fence fresh trading, signals, or protection. It may reject
  new mutations only while draining and must then wait for admitted mutations
  to finish before shutdown.
- Historical-only recovery must be structurally unable to reach execution,
  wallet, position, order, or executable-work services. Quarantined work with
  possible financial effects may be concluded automatically only from proven
  durable effect evidence (`EFFECT_COMMITTED` or `EFFECT_ABSENT`); unknown
  atomicity remains a release blocker. Binance REST rate-limit/ban handling
  must be shared by reconciliation, clock sync, bootstrap, and other REST
  callers.
- Enforce freshness from persisted candle close time before analysis and again
  before execution. Historical-only signals must be explicitly marked and
  excluded from live-latency monitoring.
- Price protection must receive the Binance observation timestamp and validate
  age/order inside the protection transaction. Paper-trading effects and their
  accepted price-event state must commit atomically; any future live-exchange
  effect needs a separate idempotent design.
- Do not add manual approval, persisted review fences, or database locks that
  block new analysis, signals, entries, or protection unless the user changes
  that direction. In-process coordination and normal database constraints are
  distinct from persisted trading fences.
- OFF-mode health/admin endpoints must not query or write retired shared-market
  tables. Existing `REVIEW_REQUIRED` history must not block OFF-mode work.
- Keep production and Replay evidence aligned. Replay must fail a parity claim
  for unresolved direct price-event evidence, while deliberate exclusions stay
  excluded.

## Delivery discipline

- Treat the local-candle architecture as not deployment-ready until its
  migration/bootstrap policy, concurrency, crash/restart, stale-input,
  Replay-parity, and Java 21/MySQL validation gates pass.
- Do not deploy or change production configuration/data unless the user
  explicitly requests it.
- Protection coverage for open positions during application replacement remains
  an unresolved release gate. Do not represent the local-candle design as
  deployment-ready until it is implemented or conclusively disproven.

## FIX-154: pending higher-timeframe BUY validation

FIX-154 is a separate, not-yet-implemented design. Do not merge it into the
local-candle change or describe it as deployed.

Purpose: a historical bearish 1h signal may block or delay a current 1m BUY
only when its own evidence is fresh, data-quality-valid, and sufficiently
strong. A historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` must remain investigatory
evidence, not a higher-timeframe validity gate; the current 1m signal's own
BTC protection remains unchanged.

- Score usable bearish 1h evidence into `NOT_TRUSTED`, `SOFT_CONFLICT`, or
  `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE` using closed-candle local indicators.
- A hard veto requires strong 1h evidence plus fresh, quality-valid bearish
  5m confirmation. Stale, invalid, or unknown historical quality can never
  hard-veto.
- Persist `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` from
  `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new signal. Historical NULL
  means unknown/invalid; never backfill it to true.
- A confirmed 1h recovery may release only a soft conflict and only with fresh,
  quality-valid, non-bearish 5m evidence.
- Preserve the legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` behavior: the new data-quality gate
  applies to the new hard-veto/recovery paths, not that legacy fallback.
- Add immutable BUY-evaluation audit evidence; maintain Replay parity and
  prevent future-candle or wall-clock look-ahead.

## AGENTS.md Protection

This file contains permanent project instructions.

Codex MUST NOT modify AGENTS.md unless the user explicitly requests
or approves the modification.

Codex may automatically maintain:
- codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md
- codex-docs/CHANGELOG_AI.md
- codex-docs/TRADING_RULES.md
- codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md

## Mandatory Documentation Maintenance

Documentation updates are part of the implementation and are NOT optional.

After every implementation, Codex MUST determine whether the change affects
architecture, trading behavior, operational behavior, or previous technical
decisions.

### Architecture changes
If the implementation changes any of the following:
- component responsibilities
- data flow
- database/table ownership
- event flow
- WebSocket usage
- service dependencies
- application boundaries
- ingestion architecture
- Replay/Production architecture

Codex MUST update:

docs/ARCHITECTURE.md

The architecture documentation must describe the FINAL implemented state,
not only what was originally planned.

### Every completed fix/change
Codex MUST update:

docs/CHANGELOG_AI.md

Include:
- date
- problem
- root cause
- solution
- files/classes changed
- database impact
- Production impact
- Replay impact

### Trading behavior changes
Codex MUST update:

docs/TRADING_RULES.md

### Investigation findings
Important investigation findings and rejected approaches MUST be recorded in:

docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md

## Definition of Done

A task is NOT COMPLETE until all required documentation has been updated.

Before finishing a task, Codex MUST explicitly check:
"Does this change require documentation updates?"

Do not wait for the user to ask for documentation updates.


## Trade investigations
When asked to investigate a trade:

- Confirm the application, environment, symbol, and trade ID.
- Use configured database and SSH connections without displaying secrets.
- Use read-only, bounded queries and targeted log searches.
- Read the actual schema before choosing tables or columns.
- Reconstruct the timeline: candle, signal, decision, entry, protection,
  and exit. Display timestamps in KSA (UTC+03:00), interpreting stored
  timestamps as UTC.
- Trace the relevant code and configuration for each decision.
- Check data freshness, entry quality, strategy, risk/reward, fees,
  stop-loss, and exit reason where applicable.
- Assess the entry using only information available at that moment.
  Use subsequent price action separately to evaluate the outcome.
- Distinguish a valid losing trade from a logic defect, stale data,
  execution delay, configuration issue, or missing evidence.
- Check whether the behavior can be reproduced in Replay.
- Support findings with record IDs, log timestamps, and code references.
- State uncertainties; do not invent a cause.
- Do not change code, configuration, services, or production data during
  an investigation unless the user requests a fix.

Report:
1. What happened.
2. Why the system entered and exited.
3. Whether the behavior followed the intended rules.
4. Confirmed defects and missing evidence.
5. Recommended correction and regression checks, if needed.

## Current architecture direction (pending implementation)

The current priority is to remove the trader's runtime dependency on the
collector. This is a design decision, not an implemented/deployed change.

- Target runtime: trader-owned Binance kline ingestion and local
  `crypto_ai.candle` writes with `shared-market.mode=OFF`.
- The collector remains independent in `crypto_ai_v2`; the trader must not
  connect to, read from, or write collector stream/cursor tables at runtime.
- Do not run direct ingestion and the shared-market executors together.
- Applied shared-market tables and Flyway history are historical evidence; do
  not drop, reset, or edit them merely to enable the new path.
- `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` still requires
  `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event` for a fresh Flyway install. Treat
  trader-only bootstrap as a release blocker until a forward, tested installer
  policy exists. Never edit an applied Flyway migration.

## Local-candle implementation safety

When implementing or reviewing the local-candle design:

- Keep `shared-market.fix142.enabled=false`,
  `shared-market.fix142.acknowledgment-enabled=false`, and scheduled analysis
  disabled for the OFF-mode cutover unless a reviewed change explicitly says
  otherwise.
- Preserve the input -> protection -> observer -> close-dispatch transaction
  order. Candle close analysis must not run on the Binance ingestion thread.
- A closed candle needs durable/recoverable analysis admission; an in-memory
  event/queue alone loses work across a crash. Recovery must distinguish fresh
  execution-capable work from historical-only analysis.
- Candle updates must be monotonic: a late open update cannot reopen or
  overwrite a closed candle. Publish close work only for an atomic
  open-to-closed transition.
- A WebSocket reconnect can occur at a candle-close boundary and Binance may
  not replay that final closed-kline event. Candle recovery is automatic: use
  an exact authoritative Binance REST candle only after exchange-time
  finality and the measured live-close grace. It may either finalise a matching
  existing `closed=0` row or insert a wholly absent identity inside the active
  data-quality window. A recovered open row preserves provenance; an inserted
  gap row uses `BACKFILL` provenance. Neither path may alter a completed row,
  create a close-transition token, dispatch analysis, create a live signal,
  or execute a trade. The recovery must have durable retry/audit evidence,
  bounded rate-limit handling, health visibility, and no manual repair step.
- Automation direction: no pending candle or analysis outcome may require
  manual approval, manual database repair, or a persisted trading fence.
  Reconciliation must retry and resolve automatically, or age out to a durable
  explicit outcome with a visible reason. This does not permit unverified data
  to masquerade as valid or force an entry: a genuine strategy/risk veto or
  unavailable authoritative data remains non-executable evidence, not a
  silently stuck pending signal.
- Gap backfill must be a pure insert-if-absent operation. It must never use a
  duplicate-key update that could finalise or alter a live row which appeared
  while the authoritative REST request was in progress. Recovery candidates
  are bounded by the active quality window and terminal audit outcomes are not
  retried. Each candle/work path must write one durable terminal analysis
  outcome; transient failures remain retryable and write no terminal outcome.
- Any future universal trading-mutation barrier is an in-memory drain
  participant only. Outside an active drain it must not block, delay, queue,
  or persistently fence fresh trading, signals, or protection. It may reject
  new mutations only while draining and must then wait for admitted mutations
  to finish before shutdown.
- Historical-only recovery must be structurally unable to reach execution,
  wallet, position, order, or executable-work services. Quarantined work with
  possible financial effects may be concluded automatically only from proven
  durable effect evidence (`EFFECT_COMMITTED` or `EFFECT_ABSENT`); unknown
  atomicity remains a release blocker. Binance REST rate-limit/ban handling
  must be shared by reconciliation, clock sync, bootstrap, and other REST
  callers.
- Enforce freshness from persisted candle close time before analysis and again
  before execution. Historical-only signals must be explicitly marked and
  excluded from live-latency monitoring.
- Price protection must receive the Binance observation timestamp and validate
  age/order inside the protection transaction. Paper-trading effects and their
  accepted price-event state must commit atomically; any future live-exchange
  effect needs a separate idempotent design.
- Do not add manual approval, persisted review fences, or database locks that
  block new analysis, signals, entries, or protection unless the user changes
  that direction. In-process coordination and normal database constraints are
  distinct from persisted trading fences.
- OFF-mode health/admin endpoints must not query or write retired shared-market
  tables. Existing `REVIEW_REQUIRED` history must not block OFF-mode work.
- Keep production and Replay evidence aligned. Replay must fail a parity claim
  for unresolved direct price-event evidence, while deliberate exclusions stay
  excluded.

## Delivery discipline

- Treat the local-candle architecture as not deployment-ready until its
  migration/bootstrap policy, concurrency, crash/restart, stale-input,
  Replay-parity, and Java 21/MySQL validation gates pass.
- Do not deploy or change production configuration/data unless the user
  explicitly requests it.
- Protection coverage for open positions during application replacement remains
  an unresolved release gate. Do not represent the local-candle design as
  deployment-ready until it is implemented or conclusively disproven.

## FIX-154: pending higher-timeframe BUY validation

FIX-154 is a separate, not-yet-implemented design. Do not merge it into the
local-candle change or describe it as deployed.

Purpose: a historical bearish 1h signal may block or delay a current 1m BUY
only when its own evidence is fresh, data-quality-valid, and sufficiently
strong. A historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` must remain investigatory
evidence, not a higher-timeframe validity gate; the current 1m signal's own
BTC protection remains unchanged.

- Score usable bearish 1h evidence into `NOT_TRUSTED`, `SOFT_CONFLICT`, or
  `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE` using closed-candle local indicators.
- A hard veto requires strong 1h evidence plus fresh, quality-valid bearish
  5m confirmation. Stale, invalid, or unknown historical quality can never
  hard-veto.
- Persist `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` from
  `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new signal. Historical NULL
  means unknown/invalid; never backfill it to true.
- A confirmed 1h recovery may release only a soft conflict and only with fresh,
  quality-valid, non-bearish 5m evidence.
- Preserve the legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` behavior: the new data-quality gate
  applies to the new hard-veto/recovery paths, not that legacy fallback.
- Add immutable BUY-evaluation audit evidence; maintain Replay parity and
  prevent future-candle or wall-clock look-ahead.

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java
   1: package com.crypto.shared;
   2: 
   3: import com.zaxxer.hikari.HikariConfig;
   4: import com.zaxxer.hikari.HikariDataSource;
   5: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
   6: import org.springframework.core.env.Environment;
   7: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   9: 
  10: /** FIX-132: separate SELECT-only pool; never register a second DataSource bean
  11:  * that would accidentally replace Boot's local Flyway/JPA datasource. */
  12: @Component
  13: public class SharedMarketSource {
  14:     private final boolean enabled;
  15:     private final String mode;
  16:     private final JdbcTemplate local;
  17:     private final JdbcTemplate reader;
  18:     private final HikariDataSource pool;
  19:     public SharedMarketSource(JdbcTemplate local, Environment env) {
  20:         mode = env.getProperty("shared-market.mode", "OFF").trim();
  21:         if (!java.util.Set.of("OFF","OBSERVE","LIVE").contains(mode)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-132 invalid shared-market.mode");
  22:         this.local=local; enabled="LIVE".equals(mode);
  23:         if ("OFF".equals(mode)) { reader = local; pool = null; return; }
  24:         HikariConfig c = new HikariConfig();
  25:         c.setJdbcUrl(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.jdbc-url"));
  26:         c.setUsername(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.username"));
  27:         c.setPassword(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.password"));
  28:         c.setMaximumPoolSize(3); c.setMinimumIdle(0); c.setReadOnly(true);
  29:         c.setConnectionTimeout(5000); c.setValidationTimeout(2000); c.setConnectionInitSql("SET time_zone = '+00:00'");
  30:         c.setPoolName("fix132-source-read");
  31:         // Finite driver waits as well as pool/query waits; no single overall deadline is claimed.
  32:         c.addDataSourceProperty("connectTimeout", "2000");
  33:         c.addDataSourceProperty("socketTimeout", "5000");
  34:         c.addDataSourceProperty("connectionTimeZone","UTC");
  35:         c.addDataSourceProperty("forceConnectionTimeZoneToSession","true");
  36:         pool = new HikariDataSource(c); reader = new JdbcTemplate(env.getProperty("diagnostics.jdbc.enabled",Boolean.class,true) ? new com.crypto.infrastructure.diagnostics.JdbcTimingDataSource(pool,"collector-read",env.getProperty("diagnostics.jdbc.slow-ms",Long.class,250L)) : pool); reader.setQueryTimeout(5);
  37:         try {
  38:             // FIX-149: validate the explicitly configured collector schema, distinct from the trader database.
  39:             String expectedSchema = env.getProperty("shared-market.source-schema", "crypto_ai_v2");
  40:             if(!expectedSchema.equals(reader.queryForObject("SELECT DATABASE()",String.class)))
  41:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 shared connection must target configured source schema " + expectedSchema);
  42:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pool.close();throw failure; }
  43:     }
  44:     public boolean enabled() { return enabled; }
  45:     public JdbcTemplate reader() { return enabled ? reader : local; }
  46:     public JdbcTemplate feedReader() { return reader; }
  47:     /** Instantaneous pool pressure, not a per-query connection-wait measurement. */
  48:     public java.util.Map<String,Integer> poolPressure() {
  49:         if(pool==null || pool.getHikariPoolMXBean()==null)return java.util.Map.of();
  50:         var bean=pool.getHikariPoolMXBean();
  51:         return java.util.Map.of("active",bean.getActiveConnections(),"idle",bean.getIdleConnections(),"waiting",bean.getThreadsAwaitingConnection());
  52:     }
  53:     public String mode() { return mode; }
  54:     public void requireLocalWriter() {
  55:         if (enabled) throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle writes; Trader import is disabled");
  56:     }
  57:     @PreDestroy public void close() { if (pool != null) pool.close(); }
  58: }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.indicator.event.CandleClosedEvent;
   4: import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
   5: import com.crypto.position.service.LivePositionProtectionService;
   6: import com.crypto.debug.monitor.service.PriceMoveMonitorService;
   7: import com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService;
   8: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
   9: 
  10: import org.springframework.context.ApplicationEventPublisher;
  11: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  12: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
  13: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation;
  14: import com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.KlineTransactionCoordinator;
  15: 
  16: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  17: import java.time.Instant;
  18: import java.util.Locale;
  19: 
  20: @Service
  21: public class BinanceKlineService {
  22:     private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(BinanceKlineService.class);
  23:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  24:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  25: 
  26:     private final KlineTransactionCoordinator transactions;
  27:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  28:     private final ApplicationEventPublisher eventPublisher;
  29:     private final LivePositionProtectionService livePositionProtectionService;
  30:     private final PriceMoveMonitorService priceMoveMonitorService;
  31:     private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
  32:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false)
  33:     private com.crypto.local.LocalDrainService drain;
  34:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false)
  35:     private com.crypto.local.LocalReconciliationAuditRepository reconciliationAudit;
  36: 
  37:     public BinanceKlineService(
  38:             CandleRepository candleRepository,
  39:             ApplicationEventPublisher eventPublisher,
  40:             LivePositionProtectionService livePositionProtectionService,
  41:             PriceMoveMonitorService priceMoveMonitorService,
  42:             MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService,
  43:             KlineTransactionCoordinator transactions
  44:     ) {
  45:         this.transactions = transactions;
  46:         this.candleRepository = candleRepository;
  47:         this.eventPublisher = eventPublisher;
  48:         this.livePositionProtectionService = livePositionProtectionService;
  49:         this.priceMoveMonitorService = priceMoveMonitorService;
  50:         this.marketPriceEventService = marketPriceEventService;
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     // FIX-124: no suspended caller transaction may retain market-data locks.
  54:     @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.NEVER)
  55:     public boolean processKline(JsonNode root) {
  56:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: Trader kline ingestion is disabled"); }
  57:         if(drain!=null && !drain.beginInput()){log.info("[FIX-153][KLINE_REJECTED_DRAINING]");return false;}
  58:         try {
  59: 
  60:         JsonNode data = root.has("data")
  61:                 ? root.path("data")
  62:                 : root;
  63: 
  64:         JsonNode kline = data.path("k");
  65: 
  66:         if (kline.isMissingNode() || kline.isNull()) {
  67:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
  68:                     "Binance message does not contain kline data"
  69:             );
  70:         }
  71: 
  72:         String symbol = kline.path("s")
  73:                 .asText()
  74:                 .trim()
  75:                 .toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  76: 
  77:         String intervalCode = kline.path("i")
  78:                 .asText()
  79:                 .trim();
  80: 
  81:         Instant openTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
  82:                 kline.path("t").asLong()
  83:         );
  84: 
  85:         Instant closeTime = Instant.ofEpochMilli(
  86:                 kline.path("T").asLong()
  87:         );
  88: 
  89:         boolean closed = kline.path("x")
  90:                 .asBoolean(false);
  91:         BigDecimal livePrice = MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(decimal(kline, "c"));
  92: 
  93:         // FIX-153: an absent Binance event timestamp is not equivalent to a fresh receipt.
  94:         Instant observedAt = data.path("E").asLong(0L)>0 ? Instant.ofEpochMilli(data.path("E").asLong()) : null;
  95:         boolean canonical="1m".equals(intervalCode);
  96:         String token=java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
  97:         java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<CandleRepository.Transition> transition=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
  98:         java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<Long> priceId=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
  99:         long started=System.nanoTime();
 100:         try {
 101:             transactions.process(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,livePrice,
 102:                 ()->{
 103:                     transition.set(candleRepository.upsertRuntime(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,
 104:                         decimal(kline,"o"),decimal(kline,"h"),decimal(kline,"l"),livePrice,
 105:                         decimal(kline,"v"),decimal(kline,"q"),kline.path("n").asLong(),decimal(kline,"V"),decimal(kline,"Q"),closed,token));
 106:                     if(canonical)priceId.set(marketPriceEventService.recordPending(symbol,livePrice,observedAt));
 107:                 },
 108:                 canonical ? ()->{if(priceId.get()!=null)livePositionProtectionService.onPrice(symbol,livePrice,observedAt,priceId.get());} : null,
 109:                 canonical ? ()->{if(priceId.get()!=null)priceMoveMonitorService.onPrice(symbol,livePrice,observedAt);} : null,
 110:                 closed ? ()->{var result=transition.get();if(result!=null && result.transitionedToClosed())
 111:                     eventPublisher.publishEvent(new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,result.closeTime()));} : null);
 112: 
 113:             var completedTransition=transition.get();
 114:             if(closed && completedTransition!=null && !completedTransition.transitionedToClosed()) {
 115:                 com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DUPLICATE_CLOSE);
 116:                 log.info("[FIX-153][DUPLICATE_CLOSE] receipt={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}, existingToken={}",
 117:                     token,symbol,intervalCode,openTime,completedTransition.token());
 118:                 // This audit is intentionally after the ingestion transaction and
 119:                 // fail-safe: an audit problem must never make a Binance receipt fail.
 120:                 if(reconciliationAudit!=null)try {reconciliationAudit.markLateLiveClose(symbol,intervalCode,openTime);}
 121:                 catch(RuntimeException ignored){log.warn("[FIX-153][LATE_LIVE_CLOSE_AUDIT_FAILED] symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}",symbol,intervalCode,openTime);}
 122:             } else if(!closed && completedTransition!=null && completedTransition.alreadyClosed()) {
 123:                 com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.LATE_OPEN_IGNORED);
 124:                 log.info("[FIX-153][LATE_OPEN_IGNORED] receipt={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}",token,symbol,intervalCode,openTime);
 125:             }
 126: 
 127:             log.debug("[FIX-153][KLINE_PHASES_RETURNED] receipt={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}, observedUtc={}, transitionedToClosed={}, elapsedIncludingTransactionsMs={}; protection outcome is in price evidence",
 128:                 token,priceId.get(),symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,transition.get()!=null&&transition.get().transitionedToClosed(),(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000);
 129:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){
 130:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PIPELINE_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED);
 131:             log.error("[FIX-153][KLINE_PHASE_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED] receipt={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, openUtc={}; inspect durable evidence, do not infer no effects",token,priceId.get(),symbol,intervalCode,openTime,failure);
 132:             throw failure;
 133:         }finally{if(priceId.get()!=null)marketPriceEventService.release(priceId.get());}
 134: 
 135:         return closed;
 136:         } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeInput();}
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     private BigDecimal decimal(
 140:             JsonNode node,
 141:             String field
 142:     ) {
 143:         String value = node.path(field).asText();
 144: 
 145:         if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
 146:             return BigDecimal.ZERO;
 147:         }
 148: 
 149:         return new BigDecimal(value);
 150:     }
 151: }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java
   1: package com.crypto.client.binance.websocket;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties;
   4: import com.crypto.service.BinanceKlineService;
   5: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
   6: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
   7: import java.net.URI;
   8: import java.time.Instant;
   9: import java.util.ArrayList;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.concurrent.*;
  12: import java.util.concurrent.locks.LockSupport;
  13: import java.util.function.Supplier;
  14: import org.slf4j.Logger;
  15: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
  16: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
  17: import org.springframework.boot.context.event.ApplicationReadyEvent;
  18: import org.springframework.context.event.EventListener;
  19: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
  20: import org.springframework.web.socket.WebSocketSession;
  21: import org.springframework.web.socket.client.standard.StandardWebSocketClient;
  22: 
  23: /** FIX-131: one owner for startup/reload/health. The actor never does database or socket I/O.
  24:  * A timed-out, unresolved handshake is retained: cancellation alone cannot prove no late socket.
  25:  * Availability is deliberately sacrificed until old work AND transport have terminated. */
  26: @Component
  27: public class BinanceWebSocketManager {
  28:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  29:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  30: 
  31:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(BinanceWebSocketManager.class);
  32:     static final long SCAN_NANOS = TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(500);
  33:     static final long DEADLINE_NANOS = TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(15);
  34:     interface Connector { CompletableFuture<WebSocketSession> connect(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler, String url); }
  35:     private final BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
  36:     private final Supplier<String> urls;
  37:     private final ObjectMapper mapper;
  38:     private final BinanceKlineService service;
  39:     private final Connector connector;
  40:     private final CandleGapDiagnostics gaps;
  41:     @Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.LocalCandleReconciliationService reconciliation;
  42:     private final java.util.function.LongSupplier nanos;
  43:     private final ArrayBlockingQueue<CompletableFuture<Void>> requests = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(32);
  44:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor connectWorker = worker("fix131-connect", 1);
  45:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor closeWorker = worker("fix131-close", 1);
  46:     private final Object lifecycle = new Object();
  47:     private final CompletableFuture<Void> stopped = new CompletableFuture<>();
  48:     private final List<CompletableFuture<Void>> waiting = new ArrayList<>();
  49:     private final java.util.Set<BinanceWebSocketHandler> cleanup = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  50:     private final java.util.Set<BinanceWebSocketHandler> cleaning = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  51:     private volatile boolean stopRequested;
  52:     private volatile Thread actor;
  53:     private volatile String phase = "STOPPED";
  54:     private volatile Attempt current;
  55:     private long sequence;
  56:     private long interruptionStart;
  57:     private Instant revokedAt;
  58:     private long nextAttempt;
  59:     private boolean replacement;
  60:     private static final class Attempt {
  61:         final BinanceWebSocketHandler handler;
  62:         final long started;
  63:         volatile boolean settled;
  64:         volatile Throwable failure;
  65:         volatile String url;
  66:         volatile boolean closing;
  67:         long nextClose;
  68:         long revoked;
  69:         boolean timeoutLogged;
  70:         long nextSilenceLog;
  71:         Attempt(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler, long started) { this.handler = handler; this.started = started; }
  72:     }
  73:     @Autowired
  74:     public BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, BinanceStreamUrlBuilder builder,
  75:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps) {
  76:         this(properties, builder::build, mapper, service,
  77:                 (handler, url) -> new StandardWebSocketClient().execute(handler, null, URI.create(url)), gaps);
  78:     }
  79:     BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, Supplier<String> urls,
  80:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, Connector connector, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps) {
  81:         this(properties, urls, mapper, service, connector, gaps, System::nanoTime);
  82:     }
  83:     BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, Supplier<String> urls,
  84:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, Connector connector, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps,
  85:             java.util.function.LongSupplier nanos) {
  86:         this.nanos = nanos;
  87:         this.properties = properties; this.urls = urls; this.mapper = mapper; this.service = service;
  88:         this.connector = connector; this.gaps = gaps;
  89:     }
  90:     private static ThreadPoolExecutor worker(String name, int queue) {
  91:         return new ThreadPoolExecutor(1, 1, 0, TimeUnit.SECONDS, new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(queue), task -> {
  92:             Thread t = new Thread(task, name); t.setDaemon(true); return t;
  93:         }, new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
  94:     }
  95:     @EventListener(ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
  96:     public void start() {
  97:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  98:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
  99:             if (actor != null || stopRequested || !properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) return;
 100:             replacement = true;
 101:             actor = new Thread(this::run, "fix131-ws-owner");
 102:             actor.setDaemon(true);
 103:             actor.start();
 104:         }
 105:     }
 106:     void wake() { LockSupport.unpark(actor); }
 107:     private void notice(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler) {
 108:         if (handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED && !handler.allClosed()) {
 109:             if (current == null || current.handler != handler) cleanup.add(handler);
 110:             // After bounded shutdown, even the unresolved CURRENT handshake retains a cleanup path.
 111:             // This fallback runs only during shutdown, never on a live processing callback.
 112:             if (closeWorker.isShutdown() && handler.gate.isDrained()) handler.close();
 113:         }
 114:         wake();
 115:     }
 116:     CompletableFuture<Void> requestReload() {
 117:         if (!properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 118:         var request = new CompletableFuture<Void>();
 119:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
 120:             if (stopRequested) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 121:             if (!requests.offer(request)) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.BUSY);
 122:         }
 123:         start(); wake();
 124:         return request;
 125:     }
 126:     public void reload() {
 127:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
 128:         var request = requestReload();
 129:         try { request.get(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS); }
 130:         catch (TimeoutException ex) { throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 131:         catch (InterruptedException ex) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 132:         catch (ExecutionException ex) {
 133:             if (ex.getCause() instanceof RuntimeException runtime) throw runtime;
 134:             throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 135:         }
 136:     }
 137:     private WebSocketReconnectException failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason reason) {
 138:         return new WebSocketReconnectException(reason, phase);
 139:     }
 140:     private void run() {
 141:         try {
 142:             while (true) {
 143:                 // Persistent lifecycle facts are examined on EVERY pass, before more queued requests.
 144:                 tick();
 145:                 if (stopRequested && current == null && cleanup.isEmpty()) break;
 146:                 LockSupport.parkNanos(SCAN_NANOS);
 147:             }
 148:             phase = "STOPPED";
 149:             finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 150:             stopped.complete(null);
 151:         } catch (Throwable ex) {
 152:             log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][OWNER_FAILED] phase={}; restart required", phase, ex);
 153:             synchronized (lifecycle) { stopRequested = true; if (current != null) current.handler.gate.revoke(); }
 154:             finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 155:             stopped.completeExceptionally(ex);
 156:         }
 157:     }
 158:     private void tick() {
 159:         long now = nanos.getAsLong();
 160:         for (var h : cleanup) {
 161:             if (h.allClosed()) { cleanup.remove(h); continue; }
 162:             if (h.gate.isDrained() && cleaning.add(h)) {
 163:                 try { closeWorker.execute(() -> {
 164:                     try { h.close(); } finally { cleaning.remove(h); wake(); }
 165:                 }); } catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) { cleaning.remove(h); }
 166:             }
 167:         }
 168:         // Bound callers retained off-mailbox as well as mailbox capacity.
 169:         CompletableFuture<Void> request;
 170:         for (int n = 0; n < 32 && (request = requests.poll()) != null; n++) {
 171:             if (waiting.size() >= 32) request.completeExceptionally(failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.BUSY));
 172:             else { waiting.add(request); replacement = true; }
 173:         }
 174:         if (current != null) {
 175:             var a = current;
 176:             var h = a.handler;
 177:             if (a.failure != null && h.gate.state() != GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) {
 178:                 log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][CONNECTION_FAILED] generation={}", h.generation, a.failure);
 179:                 finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 180:             }
 181:             if (stopRequested || replacement || h.failed || a.failure != null
 182:                     || (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.ADMITTING && !h.isConnected())) revoke(a, now);
 183:             if (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.PENDING) {
 184:                 if (now - a.started >= DEADLINE_NANOS) {
 185:                     log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][CONNECTION_TIMEOUT] generation={}; awaiting terminal handshake/closure", h.generation);
 186:                     revoke(a, now);
 187:                     finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 188:                 } else if (a.settled && h.isConnected()) {
 189:                     synchronized (lifecycle) {
 190:                         if (!stopRequested && h.gate.activate()) {
 191:                             phase = "ACTIVE";
 192:                             h.lastReceiptNanos = System.nanoTime();
 193:                             log.info("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][ADMISSION_ENABLED] generation={}, admittedAt={}, revokedAt={}, interruptionMs={}",
 194:                                     h.generation, Instant.now(), revokedAt, interruptionStart == 0 ? 0 : TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(now - interruptionStart));
 195:                             interruptionStart = 0;
 196:                             try { if (gaps != null) gaps.subscribed(a.url, Instant.now()); }
 197:                             catch (RuntimeException ex) { log.warn("[FIX-131][SUBSCRIPTION_DIAGNOSTIC_FAILED] generation={}", h.generation, ex); }
 198:                             // Request only; all local queries and REST recovery run on
 199:                             // the dedicated reconciliation executor, never this actor.
 200:                             try { if(reconciliation!=null) reconciliation.requestAfterReconnect(h.generation); }
 201:                             catch(RuntimeException ex) { log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_REQUEST_FAILED] generation={}",h.generation,ex); }
 202:                             finish(null);
 203:                         }
 204:                     }
 205:                 }
 206:             }
 207:             if (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.ADMITTING && now >= a.nextSilenceLog && System.nanoTime() - h.lastReceiptNanos > TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30)) {
 208:                 a.nextSilenceLog = now + TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30);
 209:                 log.warn("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][NO_MESSAGES] generation={}, silenceMs={}; observe only, no forced reconnect", h.generation, TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime() - h.lastReceiptNanos));
 210:             }
 211:             if (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) {
 212:                 phase = h.gate.isDrained() ? "CLOSING" : "DRAINING";
 213:                 if (now - a.revoked >= DEADLINE_NANOS && !a.timeoutLogged) {
 214:                     a.timeoutLogged = true;
 215:                     var reason = h.gate.isDrained() ? WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CLOSE_TIMEOUT : WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.DRAIN_TIMEOUT;
 216:                     log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][{}] generation={}, inFlight={}; replacement remains blocked", reason, h.generation, h.gate.inFlight());
 217:                     finish(reason);
 218:                 }
 219:                 if (h.gate.isDrained()) {
 220:                     if (!h.allClosed() && !a.closing && now >= a.nextClose) {
 221:                         a.closing = true;
 222:                         try { closeWorker.execute(() -> { try { h.close(); } finally { a.closing = false; wake(); } }); }
 223:                         catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) { a.closing = false; log.error("[FIX-131][CLOSE_WORKER_REJECTED] generation={}", h.generation); }
 224:                         a.nextClose = now + TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(5);
 225:                     }
 226:                     if (a.settled && h.allClosed() && !a.closing) {
 227:                         log.info("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][RETIRED] generation={}, drainedAt={}, rejectedMessages={}", h.generation, h.gate.drainedAt(), h.rejected.get());
 228:                         current = null;
 229:                         replacement = !stopRequested;
 230:                         nextAttempt = now + (a.failure == null ? 0 : TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(Math.max(1, properties.getWebsocket().getHealthCheckSeconds())));
 231:                     }
 232:                 }
 233:             }
 234:         }
 235:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
 236:             if (!stopRequested && current == null && cleanup.isEmpty() && replacement && now >= nextAttempt) launch();
 237:         }
 238:     }
 239:     private void revoke(Attempt a, long now) {
 240:         if (a.handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) return;
 241:         a.handler.gate.revoke();
 242:         a.revoked = now;
 243:         if (interruptionStart == 0) { interruptionStart = now; revokedAt = Instant.now(); }
 244:         log.warn("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][REVOKED] generation={}, inFlight={}", a.handler.generation, a.handler.gate.inFlight());
 245:     }
 246:     private void launch() {
 247:         replacement = false;
 248:         phase = "PENDING";
 249:         Attempt a = new Attempt(new BinanceWebSocketHandler(mapper, service, ++sequence, this::notice), nanos.getAsLong());
 250:         current = a;
 251:         log.info("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][CONNECTING] generation={}", sequence);
 252:         try {
 253:             connectWorker.execute(() -> {
 254:                 try {
 255:                     a.url = urls.get(); // DB-backed subscription snapshot is never loaded by actor.
 256:                     synchronized (lifecycle) {
 257:                         if (stopRequested || a.handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) {
 258:                             a.settled = true; wake(); return;
 259:                         }
 260:                         // execute returns a future; no waiting under the lifecycle lock.
 261:                         connector.connect(a.handler, a.url).whenComplete((session, ex) -> {
 262:                             if (session != null && a.handler.primary != session) a.handler.afterConnectionEstablished(session);
 263:                             a.failure = ex; a.settled = true; wake();
 264:                         });
 265:                     }
 266:                 } catch (Throwable ex) { a.failure = ex; a.settled = true; wake(); }
 267:             });
 268:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) { a.failure = ex; a.settled = true; }
 269:     }
 270:     private void finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason reason) {
 271:         for (var f : waiting) { if (reason == null) f.complete(null); else f.completeExceptionally(failure(reason)); }
 272:         waiting.clear();
 273:     }
 274:     boolean isStopRequested() { return stopRequested; }
 275:     public String lifecyclePhase() { return phase; }
 276:     @PreDestroy public void stop() {
 277:         synchronized (lifecycle) { stopRequested = true; }
 278:         wake();
 279:         if (actor != null) try { stopped.get(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS); }
 280:         catch (Exception ex) { log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][SHUTDOWN_INCOMPLETE] phase={}; cleanup not confirmed", phase); }
 281:         connectWorker.shutdown(); closeWorker.shutdown();
 282:     }
 283: }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataService.java
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import java.util.Comparator;
   4: import java.util.List;
   5: import java.util.Locale;
   6: 
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
   9: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
  10: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
  11: 
  12: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
  13: import com.crypto.client.binance.dto.BinanceKline;
  14: import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
  15: import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
  16: 
  17: @Service
  18: public class MarketDataService {
  19:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  20:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  21: 
  22: 
  23:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient client;
  24:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  25:     private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
  26: 
  27:     public MarketDataService(BinanceMarketDataClient client,CandleRepository candleRepository,PlatformTransactionManager manager){
  28:         this.client=client;this.candleRepository=candleRepository;this.transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  29:     }
  30: 
  31:     public int importCandles(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
  32:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle imports"); }
  33:         String normalizedSymbol = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  34:         // Do not trust a potentially skewed local clock to finalise Binance's
  35:         // currently-open REST candle. Read it before klines: a later timestamp
  36:         // could otherwise turn an in-flight response row into a false close.
  37:         java.time.Instant exchangeNow=client.getServerTime();
  38:         List<BinanceKline> klines = client.getKlines(normalizedSymbol, interval, limit);
  39:         // No database transaction is open while Binance is contacted. The local
  40:         // writes remain one transaction once the bounded network calls return.
  41:         Integer upserted=transaction.execute(status->{
  42:             int count=0;
  43:             for (BinanceKline kline : klines) {
  44:                 // FIX-153: an in-progress REST candle is not a close event or historical evidence.
  45:                 if (kline.closeTime().isAfter(exchangeNow)) continue;
  46:                 count += candleRepository.upsert(
  47:                         normalizedSymbol,interval,kline.openTime(),kline.closeTime(),
  48:                         kline.openPrice(),kline.highPrice(),kline.lowPrice(),kline.closePrice(),
  49:                         kline.volume(),kline.quoteAssetVolume(),kline.numberOfTrades(),
  50:                         kline.takerBuyBaseVolume(),kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume(),true);
  51:             }
  52:             return count;
  53:         });
  54:         if(upserted==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 local candle import transaction returned no result");
  55:         return upserted;
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  59:     public List<Candle> latestCandles(String symbol, String interval) {
  60:         return candleRepository
  61:                 .findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(
  62:                         symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), interval)
  63:                 .stream()
  64:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime))
  65:                 .toList();
  66:     }
  67: 
  68:     private Candle toEntity(String symbol, String interval, BinanceKline kline) {
  69:         return Candle.builder()
  70:                 .symbol(symbol)
  71:                 .intervalCode(interval)
  72:                 .openTime(kline.openTime())
  73:                 .closeTime(kline.closeTime())
  74:                 .openPrice(kline.openPrice())
  75:                 .highPrice(kline.highPrice())
  76:                 .lowPrice(kline.lowPrice())
  77:                 .closePrice(kline.closePrice())
  78:                 .volume(kline.volume())
  79:                 .quoteAssetVolume(kline.quoteAssetVolume())
  80:                 .numberOfTrades(kline.numberOfTrades())
  81:                 .takerBuyBaseVolume(kline.takerBuyBaseVolume())
  82:                 .takerBuyQuoteVolume(kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
  83:                 .closed(true)
  84:                 .build();
  85:     }
  86: }

   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
   4: import com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties;
   5: import com.crypto.config.TradingProperties;
   6: import com.crypto.domain.TechnicalIndicator;
   7: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   8: import com.crypto.indicator.service.TechnicalIndicatorService;
   9: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
  10: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
  11: import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;
  12: import org.springframework.boot.ApplicationArguments;
  13: import org.springframework.boot.ApplicationRunner;
  14: import org.springframework.core.annotation.Order;
  15: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
  16: 
  17: import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
  18: import java.util.List;
  19: import java.util.Set;
  20: 
  21: /**
  22:  * Ensures every configured symbol and interval has enough historical candles
  23:  * when the application starts. Existing candles are preserved and only missing
  24:  * Binance rows are inserted by MarketDataService.
  25:  */
  26: @Component
  27: @Order(10)
  28: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  29: @Slf4j
  30: public class MarketDataBootstrapService implements ApplicationRunner {
  31:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  32:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  33:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}")
  34:     private String marketMode="OFF";
  35: 
  36: 
  37:     private static final int REQUIRED_CANDLES = 300;
  38: 
  39:     private final BinanceMarketDataProperties marketDataProperties;
  40:     private final TradingProperties tradingProperties;
  41:     private final CoinConfigurationService coinConfigurationService;
  42:     private final MarketDataService marketDataService;
  43:     private final TechnicalIndicatorService technicalIndicatorService;
  44:     private final AnalysisService analysisService;
  45:     private final PaperTradingService paperTradingService;
  46:     private final TradeSignalRepository tradeSignalRepository;
  47: 
  48:     @Override
  49:     public void run(ApplicationArguments args) {
  50:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  51:         if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
  52:                 || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
  53:             log.info("Historical candle bootstrap is disabled");
  54:             return;
  55:         }
  56: 
  57:         List<String> symbols = selectSymbols();
  58:         List<String> intervals = selectIntervals();
  59:         int limit = Math.max(
  60:                 REQUIRED_CANDLES,
  61:                 Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
  62:         );
  63: 
  64:         for (String symbol : symbols) {
  65:             for (String interval : intervals) {
  66:                 bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
  67:             }
  68:         }
  69:     }
  70: 
  71: 
  72:     public void bootstrapSymbol(String rawSymbol) {
  73:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  74:         if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
  75:                 || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
  76:             log.info("Historical candle bootstrap skipped for {} because historical loading is disabled", rawSymbol);
  77:             return;
  78:         }
  79: 
  80:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase();
  81:         if (symbol.isBlank()) {
  82:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
  83:         }
  84: 
  85:         int limit = Math.max(
  86:                 REQUIRED_CANDLES,
  87:                 Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
  88:         );
  89:         for (String interval : selectIntervals()) {
  90:             bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
  91:         }
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     private void bootstrap(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
  95:         try {
  96:             int inserted = marketDataService.importCandles(symbol, interval, limit);
  97: 
  98:             log.info(
  99:                     "Historical bootstrap completed: symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}",
 100:                     symbol,
 101:                     interval,
 102:                     inserted
 103:             );
 104: 
 105:             // FIX-153: bootstrap is data only. An imported close must never seed a signal
 106:             // or execute a STARTUP trade; only this trader's runtime closes qualify in OFF.
 107:             if("OFF".equals(marketMode)){
 108:                 log.info("[FIX-153][BACKFILL_IMPORT_RETURNED] symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}, analysis=false, execution=false",symbol,interval,inserted);
 109:                 return;
 110:             }
 111:             TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
 112:                     .calculateAndPersist(symbol, interval, null)
 113:                     .orElse(null);
 114: 
 115:             if (indicator == null) {
 116:                 log.warn(
 117:                         "Initial indicator skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, reason=fewer than 210 closed candles",
 118:                         symbol,
 119:                         interval
 120:                 );
 121:                 return;
 122:             }
 123: 
 124:             // FIX-072: bootstrap can overlap with the live candle-close worker during startup.
 125:             // Never regenerate or re-execute a signal that Production already persisted for this
 126:             // exact symbol/interval/candle; the unique key remains the final database safety net.
 127:             if (tradeSignalRepository.existsBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
 128:                     indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime())) {
 129:                 log.info(
 130:                         "Initial signal skipped: already exists for symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}",
 131:                         indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime());
 132:                 return;
 133:             }
 134: 
 135:             TradeSignal signal = analysisService.analyzeForProcessing(indicator, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.STARTUP);
 136:             paperTradingService.processSignal(signal);
 137: 
 138:             log.info(
 139:                     "Initial signal generated: symbol={}, interval={}, score={}, decision={}",
 140:                     symbol,
 141:                     interval,
 142:                     signal.getTotalScore(),
 143:                     signal.getDecision()
 144:             );
 145:         } catch (Exception exception) {
 146:             log.error(
 147:                     "Historical bootstrap failed: symbol={}, interval={}",
 148:                     symbol,
 149:                     interval,
 150:                     exception
 151:             );
 152:         }
 153:     }
 154: 
 155:     private List<String> selectSymbols() {
 156:         Set<String> values = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 157:         values.addAll(coinConfigurationService.enabledSymbols());
 158:         return List.copyOf(values);
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     private List<String> selectIntervals() {
 162:         Set<String> values = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 163:         values.addAll(marketDataProperties.getIntervals());
 164:         values.addAll(tradingProperties.intervals());
 165:         return List.copyOf(values);
 166:     }
 167: }

  23:     public static final RowMapper<Candle> MAPPER = (r,n) -> Candle.builder()
  24:         .id(r.getLong("id")).symbol(r.getString("symbol")).intervalCode(r.getString("interval_code"))
  25:         .openTime(r.getTimestamp("open_time").toInstant()).closeTime(r.getTimestamp("close_time").toInstant())

  32:     private List<Candle> read(String tail,Object... args) {

  34:         List<Candle> rows=source.reader().query("SELECT * FROM candle WHERE "+tail,MAPPER,converted);
  35:         com.crypto.shared.CandleInputAudit.capture(tail,rows);
  36:         return rows;
  37:     }

  73:     public long countBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrue(String s,String i) {
