  10: /** Retry/audit evidence for local reconciliation.  PENDING is the only
  11:  * retryable state; terminal records deliberately never re-enter selection. */
  12: @Repository
  13: public class LocalReconciliationAuditRepository {
  14:     public static final String CANDLE_DATA = "CANDLE_DATA";
  15:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  16: 
  17:     public LocalReconciliationAuditRepository(JdbcTemplate jdbc) { this.jdbc = jdbc; }
  18: 
  19:     public static String candleKey(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  20:         // Canonical fixed precision prevents two textual Instant renderings from
  21:         // creating different audit identities for the same exchange candle.
  22:         return symbol + "|" + interval + "|" + openTime.toEpochMilli();
  23:     }
  24: 
  25:     public Optional<State> findCandle(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  26:         var rows = jdbc.query("""
  27:             SELECT status,attempt_count,next_attempt_at FROM local_reconciliation_audit
  28:             WHERE subject_type=? AND subject_key=?
  29:             """, (rs, n) -> new State(rs.getString(1), rs.getInt(2),
  30:                 rs.getTimestamp(3) == null ? null : rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant()),
  31:             CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime));
  32:         return rows.stream().findFirst();
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     public boolean due(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, Instant databaseNow) {
  36:         Optional<State> state = findCandle(symbol, interval, openTime);
  37:         return state.isEmpty() || ("PENDING".equals(state.get().status())
  38:             && (state.get().nextAttemptAt() == null || !state.get().nextAttemptAt().isAfter(databaseNow)));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     public void pending(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, int attempts,
  42:                         Instant nextAttempt, String reason, Instant exchangeSnapshot) {
  43:         upsert(symbol, interval, openTime, "PENDING", attempts, nextAttempt, reason, exchangeSnapshot, null, null);
  44:     }
  45: 
  46:     public void terminal(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, String status,
  47:                          String reason, Instant exchangeSnapshot, Instant binanceOpen, Instant binanceClose) {
  48:         upsert(symbol, interval, openTime, status, 0, null, reason, exchangeSnapshot, binanceOpen, binanceClose);
  49:     }
  50: 
  51:     public void markLateLiveClose(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  52:         jdbc.update("""
  53:             UPDATE local_reconciliation_audit
  54:             SET last_observation='LIVE_CLOSE_AFTER_RECOVERY',
  55:                 late_live_close_at=COALESCE(late_live_close_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
  56:             WHERE subject_type=? AND subject_key=? AND status IN ('RECOVERED','GAP_BACKFILLED')
  57:             """, CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime));
  58:     }
  59: 
  60:     private void upsert(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, String status, int attempts,
  61:                         Instant nextAttempt, String reason, Instant exchangeSnapshot,
  62:                         Instant binanceOpen, Instant binanceClose) {
  63:         jdbc.update("""
  64:             INSERT INTO local_reconciliation_audit
  65:             (subject_type,subject_key,symbol,interval_code,candle_open_time,status,failure_reason,attempt_count,
  66:              next_attempt_at,exchange_time_snapshot,binance_open_time,binance_close_time,resolved_at,created_at,updated_at)
  67:             VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
  68:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE status=VALUES(status),failure_reason=VALUES(failure_reason),
  69:              attempt_count=GREATEST(attempt_count,VALUES(attempt_count)),next_attempt_at=VALUES(next_attempt_at),
  70:              exchange_time_snapshot=VALUES(exchange_time_snapshot),binance_open_time=VALUES(binance_open_time),
  71:              binance_close_time=VALUES(binance_close_time),resolved_at=VALUES(resolved_at),updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
  72:             """, CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime), symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(openTime),
  73:             status, reason, attempts, nextAttempt == null ? null : Timestamp.from(nextAttempt),
  74:             exchangeSnapshot == null ? null : Timestamp.from(exchangeSnapshot),
  75:             binanceOpen == null ? null : Timestamp.from(binanceOpen),
  76:             binanceClose == null ? null : Timestamp.from(binanceClose),
  77:             "PENDING".equals(status) ? null : Timestamp.from(Instant.now()));
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     public record State(String status, int attempts, Instant nextAttemptAt) {}
  81: }

 303:     private SharedTradingReadiness.Reason readinessReason(RuntimeException failure) {
 304:         String message=String.valueOf(failure.getMessage());

 306:             ?SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.AUTOMATIC_ACTIVATION_WAITING:SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CUTOVER_REQUIRED;

 309:         if(message.contains("source sequence regressed"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.SOURCE_REGRESSED;

 322:     public void discover() {
 323:         if(stopped || "OFF".equals(source.mode())) return;
 324:         try {
 325:             for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) {
 326:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol))continue;
 327:                 var state=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 328:                 if(state.isEmpty()) {
 329:                     if(source.enabled()) { log.error("[FIX-132][REVIEW_REQUIRED] symbol={}; explicit cutover boundary missing",symbol); continue; }

 388:         }

 390:     public void dispatchPrices() {
 391:         if(stopped || "OFF".equals(source.mode())) return;
 392:         try {
 393:             // At most TWO submitted tasks, so newFixedThreadPool's internal queue cannot grow.
 394:             List<String> symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();
 395:             for(int checked=0;checked<symbols.size();checked++) {
 396:                 if(activePrices.size()>=2) break;
 397:                 String symbol=symbols.get(Math.floorMod(nextSymbol++,symbols.size()));
 398:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !activePrices.add(symbol))continue;
 399:                 try { prices.execute(()->{try {
 400:                     int count=fix142Enabled?jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4 LIMIT 8",symbol).size():jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4",Integer.class,symbol);
 401:                     for(int n=0;n<Math.min(8,count)&&!stopped;n++) processNext(symbol);
 402:                 } finally { activePrices.remove(symbol); }}); }
 403:                 catch(RuntimeException rejected) { activePrices.remove(symbol); throw rejected; }
 404:             }
 405:         } catch(Exception e) { log.error("[FIX-132][DISPATCH_FAILED]",e); }
 406:     }
 407:     private Map<String,Object> lockSymbol(String symbol) {
 408:         return jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? FOR UPDATE",symbol);
 409:     }
 410:     private boolean beginProtection(String symbol,long id,String attempt) {
 411:         return Boolean.TRUE.equals(transaction.execute(tx->{
 412:             var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
 413:             if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,source.enabled()))return false;
 414:             var event=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",id);
 415:             if(num(event,"phase")!=1 || !"1m".equals(event.get("interval_code")))return true;
 416:             if(event.get("protection_started_at")==null) {
 417:                 // Durable START precedes business. A process crash cannot turn an
 418:                 // already-started wallet attempt into a fresh automatic retry.
 419:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_started_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),protection_owner=?,status='PROTECTION_RUNNING' WHERE source_event_id=?",attempt,id);
 420:                 return true;
 421:             }
 422:             if(attempt.equals(event.get("protection_owner")))return true;
 423:             // Never steal/re-execute a started attempt. After 30s, fence it into
 424:             // review; a worker already in its transaction holds this row through
 425:             // commit, while one resuming later must observe REVIEW_REQUIRED.
 426:             if(time(event,"protection_started_at").plusSeconds(30).isBefore(Instant.now())) {
 427:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED',last_error='Started protection outcome requires reconciliation; no crash retry' WHERE source_event_id=?",id);
 428:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 429:                 log.error("[FIX-132][REVIEW_REQUIRED] symbol={}, event={}; previous protection attempt unconfirmed",symbol,id);
 430:             }
 431:             return false;
 432:         }));
 433:     }

  22:  * Ensures every configured symbol and interval has enough historical candles
  23:  * when the application starts. Existing candles are preserved and only missing
  24:  * Binance rows are inserted by MarketDataService.
  25:  */
  26: @Component
  27: @Order(10)
  28: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  29: @Slf4j
  30: public class MarketDataBootstrapService implements ApplicationRunner {
  31:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  32:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  33:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}")

  48:     @Override
  49:     public void run(ApplicationArguments args) {
  50:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  51:         if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
  52:                 || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
  53:             log.info("Historical candle bootstrap is disabled");
  54:             return;
  55:         }
  56: 
  57:         List<String> symbols = selectSymbols();
  58:         List<String> intervals = selectIntervals();
  59:         int limit = Math.max(
  60:                 REQUIRED_CANDLES,
  61:                 Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
  62:         );
  63: 
  64:         for (String symbol : symbols) {
  65:             for (String interval : intervals) {
  66:                 bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
  67:             }
  68:         }
  69:     }
  70:

  72:     public void bootstrapSymbol(String rawSymbol) {
  73:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  74:         if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
  75:                 || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
  76:             log.info("Historical candle bootstrap skipped for {} because historical loading is disabled", rawSymbol);
  77:             return;
  78:         }
  79: 
  80:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase();
  81:         if (symbol.isBlank()) {
  82:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
  83:         }
  84: 
  85:         int limit = Math.max(
  86:                 REQUIRED_CANDLES,
  87:                 Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
  88:         );
  89:         for (String interval : selectIntervals()) {
  90:             bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
  91:         }
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     private void bootstrap(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
  95:         try {
  96:             int inserted = marketDataService.importCandles(symbol, interval, limit);
  97: 
  98:             log.info(
  99:                     "Historical bootstrap completed: symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}",
 100:                     symbol,
 101:                     interval,
 102:                     inserted
 103:             );
 104: 
 105:             // FIX-153: bootstrap is data only. An imported close must never seed a signal
 106:             // or execute a STARTUP trade; only this trader's runtime closes qualify in OFF.
 107:             if("OFF".equals(marketMode)){
 108:                 log.info("[FIX-153][BACKFILL_IMPORT_RETURNED] symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}, analysis=false, execution=false",symbol,interval,inserted);
 109:                 return;
 110:             }
 111:             TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
 112:                     .calculateAndPersist(symbol, interval, null)
 113:                     .orElse(null);
 114: 
 115:             if (indicator == null) {
 116:                 log.warn(
 117:                         "Initial indicator skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, reason=fewer than 210 closed candles",
 118:                         symbol,
 119:                         interval
 120:                 );
 121:                 return;
 122:             }
 123: 
 124:             // FIX-072: bootstrap can overlap with the live candle-close worker during startup.
 125:             // Never regenerate or re-execute a signal that Production already persisted for this
 126:             // exact symbol/interval/candle; the unique key remains the final database safety net.
 127:             if (tradeSignalRepository.existsBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
 128:                     indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime())) {
 129:                 log.info(
 130:                         "Initial signal skipped: already exists for symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}",
 131:                         indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime());
 132:                 return;
 133:             }
 134: 
 135:             TradeSignal signal = analysisService.analyzeForProcessing(indicator, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.STARTUP);
 136:             paperTradingService.processSignal(signal);
 137: 
 138:             log.info(
 139:                     "Initial signal generated: symbol={}, interval={}, score={}, decision={}",
 140:                     symbol,
 141:                     interval,
 142:                     signal.getTotalScore(),
 143:                     signal.getDecision()
 144:             );
 145:         } catch (Exception exception) {
 146:             log.error(
 147:                     "Historical bootstrap failed: symbol={}, interval={}",
 148:                     symbol,
 149:                     interval,
 150:                     exception
 151:             );
 152:         }
 153:     }
 154: 
 155:     private List<String> selectSymbols() {
 156:         Set<String> values = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 157:         values.addAll(coinConfigurationService.enabledSymbols());
 158:         return List.copyOf(values);
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     private List<String> selectIntervals() {
 162:         Set<String> values = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 163:         values.addAll(marketDataProperties.getIntervals());
 164:         values.addAll(tradingProperties.intervals());
 165:         return List.copyOf(values);
 166:     }

  34:     @Scheduled(initialDelay=5000,fixedDelayString="${local-candle.detector-delay-ms:2000}")
  35:     public void detect(){

  44:                 int lane=Math.floorMod(nextLane++,total);
  45:                 String symbol=symbols.get(lane/intervals.size()),interval=intervals.get(lane%intervals.size());
  46:                 if(!dispatcher.idle(symbol,interval))continue;
  47:                 long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(interval)*2L);
  48:                 // Index is on open_time: include one full interval before the close-age lookback.
  49:                 Instant lowerOpen=now.minusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)+lookback);
  50:                 var candidates=jdbc.query("SELECT open_time,close_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND close_transition_token IS NOT NULL AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL') AND open_time>=? AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 4",
  51:                     (rs,n)->new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,interval,rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(lowerOpen),Timestamp.from(now));
  52:                 for(var event:candidates){
  53:                     String retryKey=key(event);
  54:                     Instant knownAnalysedUntil=analysedUntil.get(retryKey);
  55:                     if(knownAnalysedUntil!=null && knownAnalysedUntil.isAfter(now))continue;
  56:                     Retry retry=retries.get(retryKey);
  57:                     if(retry!=null && retry.nextAttempt().isAfter(now))continue;
  58:                     // A healthy analysis is terminal for detector purposes. Do this
  59:                     // before dispatcher admission so ordinary completed candles never
  60:                     // become retry/expiry noise.
  61:                     if(!missing(event)){retries.remove(retryKey);analysedUntil.put(retryKey,eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback));continue;}
  62:                     // No database fence; existing striped candle lock covers only check+handoff.
  63:                     var acquired=coordination.tryLock(symbol,interval,event.openTime());
  64:                     if(acquired.isEmpty())continue;
  65:                     try(var ignored=acquired.get()){
  66:                         if(dispatcher.submitDetected(event,()->missing(event))){
  67:                             // Admission is not completion. Retain bounded state until
  68:                             // durable signal/work evidence appears on a later pass.
  69:                             // Every re-admission consumes the same bounded retry budget;
  70:                             // a repeatedly failing worker must not execute forever.
  71:                             recordAdmission(retryKey,event,now,lookback);
  72:                             break;
  73:                         }
  74:                         defer(retryKey,event,now,"NOT_ADMITTED");
  75:                     }
  76:                 }
  77:             }
  78:             for(var iterator=retries.entrySet().iterator();iterator.hasNext();){
  79:                 var entry=iterator.next();
  80:                 // Retention is strictly after the SQL lower-open-time boundary.
  81:                 // Therefore pruning cannot make a still-detectable candle restart
  82:                 // with a fresh retry budget.
  83:                 if(entry.getValue().retainUntil().isBefore(now)){
  84:                     log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_PRUNED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=NO_LONGER_ELIGIBLE",entry.getKey(),entry.getValue().attempts());
  85:                     iterator.remove();
  86:                 }
  87:             }
  88:             analysedUntil.entrySet().removeIf(e->!e.getValue().isAfter(now));
  89:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DETECTOR_FAILED);
  90:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_FAILED] no checkpoint advanced; next bounded pass may retry",failure);}
  91:     }
  92:     private static String key(CandleClosedEvent e){return e.symbol()+"|"+e.intervalCode()+"|"+e.openTime();}
  93:     private void defer(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,String reason){
  94:         Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
  95:         long delay=Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));
  96:         long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(event.intervalCode())*2L);
  97:         Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
  98:         Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
  99:         if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
 100:             // Keep a terminal marker until this candle has aged out of the SQL
 101:             // lookback. Removing it immediately would restart the retry budget.
 102:             retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 103:             recordExpired(event,reason);
 104:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason={}",key,attempts,reason);return;
 105:         }
 106:         retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 107:         log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_DEFERRED] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}, reason={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),reason);
 108:     }
 109:     private void recordAdmission(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,long lookback){
 110:         Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
 111:         Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
 112:         Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
 113:         if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
 114:             // See defer(): this marker is deliberately retained until the query
 115:             // can no longer rediscover this candle.
 116:             retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 117:             recordExpired(event,"ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT");
 118:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT",key,attempts);
 119:             return;
 120:         }
 121:         long delay=retryDelaySeconds(attempts);
 122:         retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 123:         log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_ADMITTED_AWAITING_EVIDENCE] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay));
 124:     }
 125:     /** Strictly after the candidate predicate: open_time >= now - interval - lookback. */
 126:     private static Instant eligibilityEndsAfter(CandleClosedEvent event,long lookback){
 127:         return event.openTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(event.intervalCode())+lookback+1);
 128:     }
 129:     private static long retryDelaySeconds(int attempts){return Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));}
 130:     private record Retry(int attempts,Instant nextAttempt,Instant retryDeadline,Instant retainUntil) {}
 131:     private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){
 132:         return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;
 133:             default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 unsupported detector interval: "+interval);};
 134:     }
 135:     private boolean missing(CandleClosedEvent e){
 136:         if(outcomes!=null && outcomes.exists(e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),e.openTime()))return false;
 137:         return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?))",Integer.class,
 138:             e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()),e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()))==0;
 139:     }
 140:     private void recordExpired(CandleClosedEvent event,String reason){
 141:         if(outcomes!=null)outcomes.record(event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),"ANALYSIS_EXPIRED",reason,null,"LOCAL_DETECTOR");
 142:     }
 143: }

   9: 
  10: /** Retry/audit evidence for local reconciliation.  PENDING is the only
  11:  * retryable state; terminal records deliberately never re-enter selection. */
  12: @Repository
  13: public class LocalReconciliationAuditRepository {
  14:     public static final String CANDLE_DATA = "CANDLE_DATA";
  15:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  16: 
  17:     public LocalReconciliationAuditRepository(JdbcTemplate jdbc) { this.jdbc = jdbc; }
  18: 
  19:     public static String candleKey(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  20:         // Canonical fixed precision prevents two textual Instant renderings from
  21:         // creating different audit identities for the same exchange candle.
  22:         return symbol + "|" + interval + "|" + openTime.toEpochMilli();
  23:     }
  24: 
  25:     public Optional<State> findCandle(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  26:         var rows = jdbc.query("""
  27:             SELECT status,attempt_count,next_attempt_at FROM local_reconciliation_audit
  28:             WHERE subject_type=? AND subject_key=?
  29:             """, (rs, n) -> new State(rs.getString(1), rs.getInt(2),
  30:                 rs.getTimestamp(3) == null ? null : rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant()),
  31:             CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime));
  32:         return rows.stream().findFirst();
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     public boolean due(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, Instant databaseNow) {
  36:         Optional<State> state = findCandle(symbol, interval, openTime);
  37:         return state.isEmpty() || ("PENDING".equals(state.get().status())
  38:             && (state.get().nextAttemptAt() == null || !state.get().nextAttemptAt().isAfter(databaseNow)));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     public void pending(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, int attempts,
  42:                         Instant nextAttempt, String reason, Instant exchangeSnapshot) {
  43:         upsert(symbol, interval, openTime, "PENDING", attempts, nextAttempt, reason, exchangeSnapshot, null, null);
  44:     }
  45: 
  46:     public void terminal(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, String status,
  47:                          String reason, Instant exchangeSnapshot, Instant binanceOpen, Instant binanceClose) {
  48:         upsert(symbol, interval, openTime, status, 0, null, reason, exchangeSnapshot, binanceOpen, binanceClose);
  49:     }
  50: 
  51:     public void markLateLiveClose(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  52:         jdbc.update("""
  53:             UPDATE local_reconciliation_audit
  54:             SET last_observation='LIVE_CLOSE_AFTER_RECOVERY',
  55:                 late_live_close_at=COALESCE(late_live_close_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
  56:             WHERE subject_type=? AND subject_key=? AND status IN ('RECOVERED','GAP_BACKFILLED')
  57:             """, CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime));
  58:     }
  59: 
  60:     private void upsert(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, String status, int attempts,
  61:                         Instant nextAttempt, String reason, Instant exchangeSnapshot,
  62:                         Instant binanceOpen, Instant binanceClose) {
  63:         jdbc.update("""
  64:             INSERT INTO local_reconciliation_audit
  65:             (subject_type,subject_key,symbol,interval_code,candle_open_time,status,failure_reason,attempt_count,
  66:              next_attempt_at,exchange_time_snapshot,binance_open_time,binance_close_time,resolved_at,created_at,updated_at)
  67:             VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
  68:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE status=VALUES(status),failure_reason=VALUES(failure_reason),
  69:              attempt_count=GREATEST(attempt_count,VALUES(attempt_count)),next_attempt_at=VALUES(next_attempt_at),
  70:              exchange_time_snapshot=VALUES(exchange_time_snapshot),binance_open_time=VALUES(binance_open_time),
  71:              binance_close_time=VALUES(binance_close_time),resolved_at=VALUES(resolved_at),updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
  72:             """, CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime), symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(openTime),
  73:             status, reason, attempts, nextAttempt == null ? null : Timestamp.from(nextAttempt),
  74:             exchangeSnapshot == null ? null : Timestamp.from(exchangeSnapshot),
  75:             binanceOpen == null ? null : Timestamp.from(binanceOpen),
  76:             binanceClose == null ? null : Timestamp.from(binanceClose),
  77:             "PENDING".equals(status) ? null : Timestamp.from(Instant.now()));
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     public record State(String status, int attempts, Instant nextAttemptAt) {}
  81: }

  13: /**

  17:  * update, not only from candle-close analysis signals. Persisting that exact feed

  35:     public static final String DIRECT_SOURCE="BINANCE_KLINE_DIRECT_FIX153";
  36:     public static BigDecimal normalizePrice(BigDecimal price){
  37:         return price==null?null:price.setScale(PRICE_SCALE,java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
  38:     }
  39:     private Instant referenceNow(){Instant local=Instant.now();return exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);}
  40:     public String invalidReason(BigDecimal price,Instant observed,Instant now){
  41:         if(price==null || price.signum()<=0)return "INVALID_PRICE";
  42:         if(observed==null)return "MISSING_TIMESTAMP";
  43:         if(observed.isAfter(now.plusSeconds(futureSkew)))return "FUTURE_TIMESTAMP";
  44:         if(observed.isBefore(now.minusSeconds(maxAge)))return "STALE_PRICE";
  45:         return null;
  46:     }
  47:     /** FIX-153: called in INPUT transaction. Invalid observations never occupy slot 1.
  48:      * A duplicate-key error is classified only if the exact canonical key exists; other
  49:      * duplicate constraints/errors propagate. JDBC statement errors do not poison a JPA proxy TX. */
  50:     public Long recordPending(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observed){
  51:         BigDecimal normalizedPrice=normalizePrice(price);
  52:         Instant now=referenceNow();String invalid=invalidReason(normalizedPrice,observed,now);
  53:         if(invalid!=null){
  54:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);
  55:             Long ageMs=observed==null?null:java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis();
  56:             log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_NOT_CANONICAL] symbol={}, price={}, observedUtc={}, observationAgeMs={}, reason={}",symbol,normalizedPrice,observed,ageMs,invalid);
  57:             return null;
  58:         }
  59:         org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder keys=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();
  60:         try {

  63:                 ps.setString(1,symbol);ps.setTimestamp(2,Timestamp.from(observed));ps.setBigDecimal(3,normalizedPrice);ps.setString(4,DIRECT_SOURCE);return ps;
  64:             },keys);

  66:         }catch(org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate){

  75:     public void release(long id){inFlight.remove(id);}
  76:     /** Runs after existing symbol/position lock at READ_COMMITTED, never counts unresolved receipts. */
  77:     public boolean laterApplied(String symbol,Instant observed){
  78:         return !jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at>? AND source IN ('BINANCE_KLINE_LIVE_CLOSE',?) AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') ORDER BY observed_at DESC LIMIT 1",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),DIRECT_SOURCE).isEmpty();

  80:     /** Exact event lineage prevents a caller from completing a different receipt.
  81:      * A lost resolver race rolls back the entire effects transaction. Slot 1 is retained. */
  82:     public void finish(long id,String symbol,Instant observed,BigDecimal price,String state,String reason){
  83:         price=normalizePrice(price);
  84:         if(!org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
  85:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome requires effects transaction");
  86:         if(!java.util.Set.of("APPLIED","NON_CANONICAL").contains(state))throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 invalid effects outcome");
  87:         int n=jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status=?,outcome_reason=? WHERE id=? AND source=? AND symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND price=? AND ordering_slot=1 AND delivery_status='PENDING'",state,reason,id,DIRECT_SOURCE,symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),price);
  88:         if(n!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome ownership/lineage changed: event="+id);
  89:         org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization(){
  90:             @Override public void afterCompletion(int status){

  96:             }
  97:         });
  98:     }
  99:     /** Startup and periodic conditional resolution: no replay and no symbol-entry fence.
 100:      * The conditional update waits behind an in-flight effect TX and cannot override APPLIED.
 101:      * Runtime absence alone is insufficient; candidates must also exceed a configured bound. */
 102:     @org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled(initialDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-initial-delay-ms:5000}",fixedDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-delay-ms:5000}")
 103:     public void resolvePending(){

 105:         try {
 106:             // FIX-153: housekeeping uses its own statement timeout, without mutating the shared template.
 107:             var bounded=new JdbcTemplate(java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(jdbcTemplate.getDataSource()));bounded.setQueryTimeout(2);

 115:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){log.warn("[FIX-153][PENDING_RESOLUTION_FAILED] durable outcome unchanged; no live fence introduced",failure);}
 116:     }

 148:     /** FIX-132: known skipped/unconfirmed collector delivery must not silently
 149:      * become legacy candle-price fallback. Historical pre-collector windows retain
 150:      * their established fallback. This reads immutable evidence, never live claims. */
 151:     public void assertReplayEvidence(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
 152:         Long pending=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",
 153:             Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 154:         if(pending!=null && pending>0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 Replay contains unconfirmed Production price outcomes; reconcile before claiming parity");
 155:         // FIX-153 OFF Replay never queries collector-era activation tables.
 156:         if("OFF".equals(mode))return;
 157:         Long cutovers=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at<=?",Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(to));
 158:         if(cutovers!=null && cutovers>0) {

   9: /** FIX-127 recovery gate only. Uses actual closed candle lineage, never generated_at
  10:  * or a nearest candle. Grace periods match FIX-043's existing recovery contract. */
  11: @Service
  12: public class SignalProcessingFreshness {
  13:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
  14:     private com.crypto.shared.SharedMarketSource sharedCandleSource;
  15:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false)
  16:     private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  17:     private org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate candleReader(org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate legacy) {
  18:         return sharedCandleSource == null ? legacy : sharedCandleSource.reader();
  19:     }
  20: 
  21:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  22:     public SignalProcessingFreshness(JdbcTemplate jdbc) { this.jdbc=jdbc; }
  23:     /** Production clock comes from the database session, which startup requires to be UTC. */
  24:     public boolean eligible(SignalProcessingStore.Work work) {
  25:         Timestamp databaseNow=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)",Timestamp.class);
  26:         if(databaseNow==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 database did not return current timestamp");
  27:         Instant reference=databaseNow.toInstant();
  28:         if(exchangeClock!=null)reference=exchangeClock.correctDatabase(reference);
  29:         return eligible(work,reference);
  30:     }
  31:     public boolean eligible(SignalProcessingStore.Work work, Instant now) {
  32:         var candles=candleReader(jdbc).query("""
  33:                 SELECT open_time,close_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=?
  34:                 AND closed=1 AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time DESC LIMIT 1
  35:                 """,(rs,n)->new Instant[]{rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()},
  36:                 work.symbol(),work.interval(),Timestamp.from(now));
  37:         if(candles.isEmpty() || !candles.get(0)[0].equals(work.candleOpenTime()))return false;
  38:         return !candles.get(0)[1].plus(grace(work.interval())).isBefore(now);
  39:     }
  40:     static Duration grace(String interval) {
  41:         return switch(interval) {
  42:             case "1m" -> Duration.ofSeconds(90);
  43:             case "5m" -> Duration.ofMinutes(2);
  44:             case "1h" -> Duration.ofMinutes(5);
  45:             case "4h" -> Duration.ofMinutes(10);
  46:             case "1d" -> Duration.ofMinutes(30);
  47:             default -> Duration.ofMinutes(2);
  48:         };
  49:     }
  50: }

md\FIX-124.md:24:These are Production transaction/recovery changes. A previously failed initial check can now succeed, so actual executions can differ during contention. Input becomes visible to other database readers earlier, and a protection commit is retained even if the later observer or dispatch fails. The split no longer provides one all-or-nothing kline/protection transaction. A process crash after input commit but before protection/dispatch can leave committed input without that evaluation; durable exactly-once tick processing is not introduced by this patch. md\FIX-130.md:7:FIX-129 captured 68 finalizations across four rollovers (17 symbols each). Summed BLOCK_FINALIZATION durations were 34m51s, 26m22s, 40m39s and 57m44s. ENA took 421,304 ms inside an observer transaction taking 421,337 ms. This establishes the blocking operation; individual internal query cost remains unmeasured. Those totals exclude the enclosing observer measurements to avoid double counting.
md\FIX-130.md:28:One is deliberate: observed calls already take minutes on shared tables. Raising concurrency could increase pressure on wallet processing. This patch isolates the work, not optimizes its queries. The historical transaction has no new execution deadline; a JDBC timeout alone would not prove a stopped server-side query or justify takeover.
md\FIX-130.md:30:This implements conservative ownership, not an automatically expiring execution lease. A claim older than 30 minutes is marked REVIEW_REQUIRED when another poll observes it; the gate stays occupied. In a single process, its busy worker cannot simultaneously poll, so a hung call may remain FINALIZING with increasing visible age. A second instance or restarted process detects stale ownership. No elapsed time alone starts a replacement.
md\FIX-130.md:40:During enqueue failure, the active tracker stays on the prior block with ROLLOVER_PERSIST_FAILED, retaining incoming observer points. The existing coordinator catches the observer failure after input/protection, so close-event dispatch still follows the existing failure-isolation path. This failure may still delay ingestion up to existing connection acquisition / bounded enqueue query timing; zero database delay is not promised.
md\FIX-130.md:42:The retry buffer is explicitly bounded at 4,096 points per symbol. At exhaustion, the incoming observer point is not retained; REVIEW_REQUIRED, unrecordedPoints and [OBSERVER_POINT_UNRECORDED] make the gap explicit. Existing buffered points are still retried, so buffer exhaustion does not permanently prevent recovery. It never drops a market-input write or suppresses protection. The unrecorded-point count remains visible for that tracker session. A finalization status reports workflow completion, not proof that every observer point was captured.
md\FIX-130.md:50:1. Retain the current point in the observer retry buffer.
md\FIX-130.md:55:6. Persist the current live catches through the unchanged observer transaction.
md\FIX-130.md:59:The guarantee is completed-block handoff, not total observer crash durability: uncommitted tracker data and retained points remain in memory and can be lost at process termination. Existing settings updates clear trackers as before. Observer point semantics continue to use the supplied observation time; no candle identity is invented for block work.
md\FIX-130.md:90:- Before deployment: run full Maven tests on Java 21, validate V88 on MySQL, exercise contention with two instances, crash/unknown-owner recovery, and a complete live rollover. Verify observer latency and no skipped candle events on actual measurements.
md\FIX-130.md:91:- No live speedup, complete ingestion coverage or Replay parity run claimed. FIX-129 is retained for before/after measurement. Background BLOCK_FINALIZATION now has thread fix130-finalizer and is no longer nested inside the live observer measurement.
md\FIX-132-candle-read-inventory.md:40:All repository consumers above now use the JDBC CandleRepository facade. Every direct-read path listed above uses SharedMarketSource, including SystemHealthDailyService's query held in a SQL variable. LIVE selects crypto_ai_v2; OFF/OBSERVE retain the local source. Historical Flyway checksums were preserved.
md\FIX-132-candle-read-inventory.md:42:Candle is no longer a JPA entity, so Hibernate validation does not require the renamed local candle table. Recovery joins are split into shared candle reads and local indicator/signal identity checks. Local upsert remains guarded and is unavailable in LIVE. Bootstrap/import/kline processing and WS start/reload are all guarded. Coin pair validation still makes a separate REST request; it is not a candle ingestion path.
md\FIX-132-candle-read-inventory.md:44:MarketPriceEventService retains provenance and applied status. ExecutionPriceAuthority reports the actual source. md\FIX-132.md:1:# FIX-132 — collector sole-source candidate
md\FIX-132.md:3:Status: implemented candidate, OFF by default. No server deployment, database migration or table rename was performed. md\FIX-132.md:7:The source collector must already publish the FIX-132 immutable sequenced feed. md\FIX-132.md:9:Trader uses an independent SELECT-only connection to crypto_ai_v2, with its own local delivery state in crypto_ai. OFF retains existing readers and ingestion. OBSERVE reads the immutable feed for diagnostics while retaining old candle readers and ingestion, and applies no feed business effects. LIVE routes repository and direct candle reads to the collector, disables Trader candle import/bootstrap/kline processing and WS startup/reload, and consumes collector prices and closes. There is no local candle fallback. Unrelated APIs such as pair validation and order-book data are outside this migration.
md\FIX-132.md:11:Input evidence, protection, observer and analysis registration have separate local transactions. Source reads finish before local transactions begin; no XA is introduced. Discovery copies source payload and advances its checkpoint atomically locally, recovering by source sequence after rollback. Each effect phase holds its local symbol/event fence through commit. This is not exactly-once wallet execution across every existing transaction boundary.
md\FIX-132.md:13:A durable protection START prevents automatic replay of a crashed or unconfirmed wallet attempt. Only the existing InitialPositionLockDeadlock pre-effect retry remains. Unknown protection results require review and stop the symbol. Observer failure is persisted without repeatedly running it or stopping later price protection. Started analysis is never automatically reassigned; a five-minute threshold marks REVIEW_REQUIRED while allowing the original owner to report completion. FIX-125 now supplies the separate transaction-lifetime wallet coordination in this integrated candidate; see FIX-125.md for the exact mechanism and test limits.
md\FIX-132.md:21:Replay never claims live feed work. OLD/NEW candle readers use shared current history in LIVE mode; price evidence uses LEGACY or APPLIED outcomes in historical order. Pending collector evidence or missing collector-era prices produce an explicit error rather than silently substituting signal prices. Pre-collector fallback remains. verifyEventResolution() and Shadow-write behavior are retained. Real-window parity remains an activation gate.
md\FIX-132.md:27:FIX-tagged lifecycle, lag, source-unavailable, sequence-gap and review logs are searchable. Proven / Analyze Trades includes a persisted delivery diagnostic panel. `/api/administration/shared-market` exposes status, bounded event windows and input fingerprints. Empty results do not establish complete coverage. Collector timer `fix132.collector.transaction` spans publisher-containing method entry through transaction completion, excluding pool acquisition/proxy begin. Feed received_at is publisher-entry time, not exact socket receipt; source created_at precedes commit. UI latency from source_created_at is not an exact post-commit transport measurement.

   1: package db.migration;
   2: 
   3: import java.sql.Connection;
   4: import java.sql.PreparedStatement;

  13: 
  14: /** FIX-149: source-window indexes in the confirmed collector schema crypto_ai_v2.
  15:  * Trader Flyway history remains in crypto_ai on this same MySQL server.
  16:  * Java migration avoids temporary stored procedures and their extra privileges.
  17:  * Existing manually-created indexes are accepted only with the exact definition. */
  18: public class V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes extends BaseJavaMigration {
  19:     private static final System.Logger LOG = System.getLogger("FIX149.SourceIndexes");
  20:     private static final String SOURCE_SCHEMA = "crypto_ai_v2";
  21:     private static final String TABLE = "market_data_stream_event";
  22:     private static final String OBSERVED = "idx_stream_fresh_observed";
  23:     private static final String CLOSED = "idx_stream_fresh_closed";
  24:     private static final List<String> OBSERVED_PARTS = List.of(
  25:         "symbol:A", "interval_code:A", "source:A", "classification:A", "observed_at:A", "symbol_sequence:A");
  26:     private static final List<String> CLOSED_PARTS = List.of(
  27:         "symbol:A", "interval_code:A", "closed:A", "source:A", "classification:A", "candle_close_time:D", "symbol_sequence:A");
  28: 
  29:     // MySQL online DDL commits implicitly; never claim transaction rollback can undo it.
  30:     @Override public boolean canExecuteInTransaction() { return false; }
  31: 
  32:     @Override public void migrate(Context context) throws Exception {
  33:         Connection connection = context.getConnection();
  34:         if (!"MySQL".equals(connection.getMetaData().getDatabaseProductName())
  35:                 || connection.getMetaData().getDatabaseMajorVersion() < 8) {
  36:             throw new SQLException("FIX-149 requires MySQL 8 with descending-index support");
  37:         }
  38:         try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement(); ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery("SELECT DATABASE()")) {
  39:             if (!result.next() || !"crypto_ai".equals(result.getString(1))) {
  40:                 throw new SQLException("FIX-149 Flyway history must run in crypto_ai; index target is explicitly crypto_ai_v2");
  41:             }
  42:         }
  43:         try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
  44:                 "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema=? AND table_name=? AND engine='InnoDB'")) {
  45:             statement.setString(1, SOURCE_SCHEMA);
  46:             statement.setString(2, TABLE);
  47:             try (ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery()) {
  48:                 if (!result.next() || result.getInt(1) != 1) {
  49:                     throw new SQLException("FIX-149 requires existing crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event on the trader MySQL server InnoDB table; no empty replacement is created");
  50:                 }
  51:             }
  52:         }
  53:         List<String> additions = new ArrayList<>();
  54:         if (!existingIndexMatches(connection, OBSERVED, OBSERVED_PARTS)) {
  55:             additions.add("ADD INDEX " + OBSERVED + " (symbol,interval_code,source,classification,observed_at,symbol_sequence)");
  56:         }
  57:         if (!existingIndexMatches(connection, CLOSED, CLOSED_PARTS)) {
  58:             additions.add("ADD INDEX " + CLOSED + " (symbol,interval_code,closed,source,classification,candle_close_time DESC,symbol_sequence ASC)");
  59:         }
  60:         if (additions.isEmpty()) {
  61:             LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEXES_ALREADY_PRESENT] schema=crypto_ai_v2 historySchema=crypto_ai utc=" + Instant.now());
  62:             return;
  63:         }
  64:         long previousLockWait;
  65:         try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement(); ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery("SELECT @@SESSION.lock_wait_timeout")) {
  66:             if (!result.next()) throw new SQLException("FIX-149 cannot read metadata-lock timeout");
  67:             previousLockWait = result.getLong(1);
  68:         }
  69:         Instant start = Instant.now();
  70:         long startNanos = System.nanoTime();
  71:         LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEX_BUILD_START] schema=crypto_ai_v2 historySchema=crypto_ai table=" + TABLE + " startUtc=" + start);
  72:         // One ALTER builds both missing indexes. Ten seconds bounds metadata-lock wait,
  73:         // not the build duration, resource use, or every possible InnoDB wait.
  74:         try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement()) {
  75:             statement.execute("SET SESSION lock_wait_timeout=10");
  76:             try {
  77:                 statement.execute("ALTER TABLE `" + SOURCE_SCHEMA + "`.`" + TABLE + "`" + " " + String.join(", ", additions) + ", ALGORITHM=INPLACE, LOCK=NONE");
  78:                 LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEX_BUILD_END] startUtc=" + start + " endUtc=" + Instant.now()
  79:                     + " durationMs=" + ((System.nanoTime() - startNanos) / 1_000_000) + " outcome=SUCCESS");
  80:             } catch (SQLException failure) {
  81:                 LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.ERROR, "[FIX-149][INDEX_BUILD_END] startUtc=" + start + " endUtc=" + Instant.now()
  82:                     + " durationMs=" + ((System.nanoTime() - startNanos) / 1_000_000) + " outcome=FAILED sqlState=" + failure.getSQLState());
  83:                 throw failure;
  84:             } finally {
  85:                 statement.execute("SET SESSION lock_wait_timeout=" + previousLockWait);
  86:             }
  87:         }
  88:     }
  89: 
  90:     static boolean existingIndexMatches(Connection connection, String name, List<String> expected) throws SQLException {
  91:         List<String> actual = new ArrayList<>();
  92:         try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
  93:                 "SELECT column_name,collation,sub_part,non_unique,index_type,is_visible FROM information_schema.statistics "
  94:                 + "WHERE table_schema=? AND table_name=? AND index_name=? ORDER BY seq_in_index")) {
  95:             statement.setString(1, SOURCE_SCHEMA);
  96:             statement.setString(2, TABLE);
  97:             statement.setString(3, name);
  98:             try (ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery()) {
  99:                 while (result.next()) {
 100:                     if (result.getObject("sub_part") != null || result.getInt("non_unique") != 1
 101:                             || !"BTREE".equals(result.getString("index_type")) || !"YES".equals(result.getString("is_visible"))) {
 102:                         throw new SQLException("FIX-149 incompatible existing index " + name + "; inspect before deployment");
 103:                     }
 104:                     actual.add(result.getString("column_name") + ":" + result.getString("collation"));
 105:                 }
 106:             }
 107:         }
 108:         if (actual.isEmpty()) return false;
 109:         if (!actual.equals(expected)) throw new SQLException("FIX-149 incompatible existing index " + name + "; expected " + expected + " found " + actual);
 110:         return true;
 111:     }
 112: }

  19:     public static String candleKey(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  20:         // Canonical fixed precision prevents two textual Instant renderings from
  21:         // creating different audit identities for the same exchange candle.
  22:         return symbol + "|" + interval + "|" + openTime.toEpochMilli();
  23:     }

  25:     public Optional<State> findCandle(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  26:         var rows = jdbc.query("""
  27:             SELECT status,attempt_count,next_attempt_at FROM local_reconciliation_audit
  28:             WHERE subject_type=? AND subject_key=?
  29:             """, (rs, n) -> new State(rs.getString(1), rs.getInt(2),
  30:                 rs.getTimestamp(3) == null ? null : rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant()),
  31:             CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime));
  32:         return rows.stream().findFirst();
  33:     }

  35:     public boolean due(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, Instant databaseNow) {

  38:             && (state.get().nextAttemptAt() == null || !state.get().nextAttemptAt().isAfter(databaseNow)));
  39:     }

  68:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE status=VALUES(status),failure_reason=VALUES(failure_reason),
  69:              attempt_count=GREATEST(attempt_count,VALUES(attempt_count)),next_attempt_at=VALUES(next_attempt_at),
  70:              exchange_time_snapshot=VALUES(exchange_time_snapshot),binance_open_time=VALUES(binance_open_time),
  71:              binance_close_time=VALUES(binance_close_time),resolved_at=VALUES(resolved_at),updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
  72:             """, CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime), symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(openTime),
  73:             status, reason, attempts, nextAttempt == null ? null : Timestamp.from(nextAttempt),
  74:             exchangeSnapshot == null ? null : Timestamp.from(exchangeSnapshot),
  75:             binanceOpen == null ? null : Timestamp.from(binanceOpen),
  76:             binanceClose == null ? null : Timestamp.from(binanceClose),
  77:             "PENDING".equals(status) ? null : Timestamp.from(Instant.now()));

 468:      */
 469:     private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
 470:         Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
 471:         Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
 472:         int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
 473:         for (String sym : symbols) {
 474:             for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
 475:                 List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 476:                         sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
 477:                 java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
 478:                 List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
 479:                         .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 480:                                 sym, interval, start, end);
 481:                 List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
 482:                 merged.addAll(prefix);
 483:                 Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
 484:                 forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
 485:                 candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
 486:                 log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
 487:                         sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
 488:             }
 489:         }
 490: 
 491:         Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
 492:         // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
 493:         // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
 494:         // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
 495:         // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().
 496:         for (TradeSignal signal : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndCandleOpenTimeBetweenOrderByCandleOpenTimeAsc(symbol, start, end)) {
 497:             ReplayDataset.SignalKey key = new ReplayDataset.SignalKey(
 498:                     signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), signal.getCandleOpenTime());
 499:             Long previous = lineage.putIfAbsent(key, signal.getId());
 500:             if (previous != null && !previous.equals(signal.getId())) {
 501:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
 502:                         + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
 503:             }
 504:         }
 505:         log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
 506:         return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
 507:     }

   1: # Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Architecture (Claude)
   2: 
   3: Maintained by Claude. Based on a read-only review of the working tree on
   4: 2026-10-09 (branch `master`, including uncommitted FIX-153 changes). Line
   5: numbers are approximate. `codex-docs/` is maintained separately by Codex.
   6: 
   7: Status labels: **Current** (in the working tree), **Pending** (design only),
   8: **Legacy/shared** (retained shared-market path).
   9: 
  10: ## Release status
  11: 
  12: - FIX-153 (trader-owned local candles, `shared-market.mode=OFF`): implemented
  13:   in source, **not approved for production**. Java 21 build/tests, disposable
  14:   MySQL bootstrap, crash/race, Windows two-process, soak and Replay-parity
  15:   gates are open. This workstation has Java 17 and cannot compile the project.
  16: - FIX-153 work is **uncommitted**: ~67 modified tracked files plus untracked
  17:   `com/crypto/local/`, `com/crypto/infrastructure/diagnostics/`, migrations
  18:   `V99`ƒ?"`V106` (except the Java `V103`) and `B105`.
  19: - FIX-154 (higher-timeframe BUY validation): pending, not implemented.
  20: 
  21: ## Stack
  22: 
  23: Spring Boot 4.1.0, Java 21, MySQL 8 (`docker-compose.yml`: `mysql:8.4`,
  24: host port 3307), Flyway (`V1`ƒ?"`V106`, plus baseline `B105`), Spring Security
  25: form login, Jenkins + `scripts/deploy/Deploy-Trader.ps1`. Paper trading only;
  26: no live-exchange orders.
  27: 
  28: ## Databases
  29: 
  30: | Schema | Owner | Trader use |
  31: |---|---|---|
  32: | `crypto_ai` | Trader | Only runtime DB in OFF mode. |
  33: | `crypto_ai_v2` | Collector | Read in LIVE/OBSERVE via `SharedMarketSource` read-only pool. Must not be touched in OFF mode. |
  34: 
  35: `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` (Java migration) adds indexes to
  36: `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`; a fresh trader-only install fails
  37: without that schema. Release blocker; do not edit applied migrations.
  38: 
  39: ## Ingestion modes (`shared-market.mode`, default `OFF`)
  40: 
  41: - **OFF (FIX-153)** ƒ?" trader connects to Binance and writes `crypto_ai.candle`.
  42:   `LocalTraderInstance` requires an absolute `local-candle.instance-lock-path`
  43:   (env `LOCAL_TRADER_INSTANCE_LOCK`) and holds an OS file lock: one trader
  44:   process per host. Empty value fails OFF-mode startup.
  45: - **LIVE / OBSERVE (legacy/shared)** ƒ?" `SharedMarketConsumer` polls collector
  46:   stream tables into local `shared_market_event_delivery`;
  47:   `SharedAnalysisDispatcher` drives analysis. Direct ingestion entry points
  48:   (`BinanceWebSocketManager`, `BinanceKlineService.processKline`,
  49:   `MarketDataService.importCandles`, `MarketDataBootstrapService`) are
  50:   disabled in LIVE. The two paths must never run together.
  51:

  53: 
  54: ```text
  55: Binance WS (combined stream, enabled coins x 1m/5m/1h)

   3: Status labels used in this document:
   4: 
   5: - **Current** ƒ?" present in the repository today.
   6: - **Pending** ƒ?" approved design direction, not implemented or deployed.
   7: - **Legacy/shared** ƒ?" existing shared-market path retained as historical evidence
   8:   while the trader moves to local-candle ownership.
   9: 
  10: ## Current release status
  11: 
  12: **FIX-153 is implemented in source but is NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION.**
  13: It moves the trader toward collector-free, trader-owned local candle ingestion
  14: in `shared-market.mode=OFF`. Java 21, disposable-MySQL, crash/race, Windows
  15: two-process, soak, OFF-mode and Replay-parity proof gates remain open.
  16: 
  17: FIX-154 is a separate pending higher-timeframe design. It is not implemented
  18: and is not part of FIX-153 deployment scope.
  19: 
  20: ## System purpose
  21: 
  22: The application is a Spring Boot cryptocurrency trader that analyses Binance
  23: market data, persists candles and technical indicators, creates trade signals,
  24: and performs paper-wallet execution and position protection. Production and
  25: Replay are intended to use equivalent persisted market evidence.
  26: 
  27: ## Runtime and data ownership
  28: 
  29: | Area | Current ownership | Target direction |
  30: |---|---|---|
  31: | Trader database | `crypto_ai` | Remains the trader's only runtime database. |
  32: | Collector database | `crypto_ai_v2` | Collector remains separate and is not read/written by the trader at runtime. |
  33: | Candle ingestion | In OFF mode, trader-owned Binance ingestion writes local `crypto_ai.candle`. | Must be proven in a collector-free deployment rehearsal. |
  34: | Candle processing | Bounded lanes, close-transition tokens and a local detector are implemented. | MySQL/race/crash proof remains required. |
  35: | Trading | Paper wallet and managed positions. | Live-exchange orders are outside the local-candle automatic-resolution design. |
  36: 
  37: ## Current primary flow
  38: 
  39: ```text
  40: Binance WebSocket kline
  41:   -> BinanceKlineService
  42:   -> KlineTransactionCoordinator
  43:       -> input transaction: candle + canonical 1m market price event
  44:       -> protection transaction: LivePositionProtectionService
  45:       -> observer transaction
  46:       -> close-dispatch transaction
  47:   -> CandleClosedEvent (AFTER_COMMIT)
  48:   -> CandleAnalysisDispatcher
  49:   -> CandleClosedAnalysisWorker
  50:   -> TechnicalIndicatorService
  51:   -> AnalysisService / TradeSignal
  52:   -> SignalProcessingCoordinator
  53:   -> PaperTradingService / wallet and managed-position state
  54: ```
  55: 
  56: `KlineTransactionCoordinator` preserves input -> protection -> observer ->
  57: close-dispatch ordering. Closed-candle analysis is asynchronous and must not
  58: run on the Binance ingestion thread.
  59: 
  60: ## Current core persistence
  61: 
  62: | Table/domain | Purpose |
  63: |---|---|
  64: | `candle` | Local OHLCV candle history keyed by symbol, interval, and open time. |
  65: | `technical_indicator` | Persisted indicator snapshots derived from closed candles. |
  66: | `trade_signal` | Persisted analysis decisions and their context. |
  67: | `signal_processing_work` | Durable processing registration/status for a persisted signal. |
  68: | `market_price_event` | Canonical 1m price evidence used by protection and Replay. |
  69: | Paper wallet / position tables | Paper executions, open-position state, TP/SL/profit protection. |
  70: 
  71: ## Legacy/shared-market path
  72: 
  73: **Current but pending retirement from the trader runtime.**
  74: 
  75: The shared path includes `SharedMarketSource`, `SharedMarketConsumer`,
  76: `SharedAnalysisDispatcher`, cutover/readiness logic, and local historical
  77: tables such as `shared_market_event_delivery`, `shared_market_consumer_state`,
  78: and `shared_market_health`. These records remain evidence and must not be
  79: dropped, reset, or used to manufacture an approval.
  80: 
  81: The collector event/cursor source is `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`
  82: and `market_data_stream_cursor`. In the target local-candle runtime, the
  83: trader must not connect to, read, or write these collector tables.
  84: 
  85: `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` is an existing Java Flyway migration
  86: that requires the collector event table for a fresh installation. A trader-only
  87: bootstrap policy is therefore **pending** and remains a release blocker. Do
  88: not edit applied Flyway migrations or Flyway history.
  89: 
  90: ## Implemented, unverified: local-candle architecture (FIX-153)
  91: 
  92: The implemented intended mode is `shared-market.mode=OFF` with trader-owned Binance kline
  93: ingestion and local candle writes. The shared executors and direct-ingestion
  94: path must never run together.
  95: 
  96: Implemented safeguards:
  97: 
  98: - Candle writes are monotonic: a late open update cannot reopen or overwrite a
  99:   closed candle.
 100: - A closed-candle transition is admitted exactly once using an atomic upsert
 101:   result plus in-process admission coordination.
 102: - A bounded local detector recovers a closed candle that was committed before
 103:   the in-memory event/dispatcher could start analysis.
 104: - Fresh candles may execute; stale candles are analysed historically only, or
 105:   their already-owned work is expired without business execution.
 106: - Analysis lanes are bounded. Under overload, 1m may retain only the latest
 107:   pending candle; higher intervals use an explicit bounded FIFO policy.
 108: - Historic shared `REVIEW_REQUIRED` evidence must not block OFF-mode work.
 109: - OFF-mode health/admin endpoints must use local-candle health and must not
 110:   query retired shared-market tables.
 111: - Current REST backfill can finalise an interrupted open row but cannot mint a
 112:   runtime close token or dispatch analysis. Runtime close tokens plus
 113:   provenance keep backfill out of detector admission. The approved, pending
 114:   automatic-reconciliation extension will also insert an exact wholly absent
 115:   candle only inside the active quality window, with `BACKFILL` provenance and
 116:   the same no-token/no-dispatch boundary.
 117: - Observed local behavior (2026-10-09): a WebSocket reconnect at a candle-close
 118:   boundary can leave the already-persisted 1m/5m rows open because Binance does
 119:   not replay the final closed-kline event. The current detector recovers missing
 120:   analysis admission, not missing close facts. An authoritative, bounded REST
 121:   repair may therefore finalise the exact `closed=0` row or, under the approved
 122:   gap-backfill scope, insert an exact absent identity. It must preserve existing
 123:   provenance, use `BACKFILL` for an insertion, create no close-transition token,
 124:   and never dispatch historical work. Automatic reconciliation of this reconnect
 125:   case remains a FIX-153 release gate; the one-time local repair is evidence,
 126:   not proof that the gap is closed.
 127: - Approved pending reconciliation design: gap backfill is insert-only (no
 128:   duplicate-key update); it can never alter a live row that appears during a
 129:   REST request. Candidate selection is bounded to the active quality window;
 130:   terminal audit records are not retried. Every candle/work route must produce
 131:   a durable terminal outcome or remain explicitly retryable after a transient
 132:   failure. Historical-only processing has no execution authority.
 133: - The pending universal mutation barrier is an in-memory drain participant,
 134:   not a database or persistent trading fence. Outside drain it must not delay,
 135:   queue, or block fresh trading, signals, or protection. Open-position
 136:   protection during application replacement remains an unresolved release gate.
 137: - Local source now contains the first reconciliation implementation slice:
 138:   V107 durable audit/outcome tables, update-only authoritative open-row repair,
 139:   pure insert-only gap backfill, a dedicated recovery executor, a reconnect
 140:   request hook, and drain participation. It is disabled by default and remains
 141:   unvalidated on MySQL; it has no deployment approval.
 142: - Direct prices are normalised to `DECIMAL(30,12)` before persistence,
 143:   protection and completion. Equal timestamps have one canonical receipt.
 144: - Every processing attempt is freshness-checked after normal locks using the
 145:   database-to-exchange correction. JVM-origin timestamp decisions use the
 146:   separately measured JVM-to-exchange correction. The measurement performs no
 147:   HTTP under execution locks and uses a bounded five-second time-only client.
 148:   General Binance REST calls use the configured request timeout, and scheduled
 149:   clock sampling has a four-thread Spring scheduler pool.
 150:   Bootstrap obtains time/klines before opening its local candle-write
 151:   transaction, so a stalled REST request does not retain database resources.
 152:   REST bootstrap gets Binance server time before klines.
 153: - An ADMIN-only drain stops analysis admission, exits the JVM on successful
 154:   quiesce, and resumes admission on timeout. Price protection pauses during
 155:   the controlled drain window.
 156: 
 157: ## Implemented, unverified: protection price-event state machine
 158: 
 159: For direct 1m price observations, the target evidence lifecycle is:
 160: 
 161: ```text
 162: PENDING
 163:   -> APPLIED                 (protection effects and state commit together)
 164:   -> NON_CANONICAL           (invalid, stale, out-of-order, or duplicate)
 165:   -> PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED  (confirmed rollback/crash resolution)

src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java:127:        if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }

src/main/java/com/crypto/local\LocalTraderInstance.java:13:public class LocalTraderInstance {
src/main/java/com/crypto/local\LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:43:    @Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode;
src/main/java/com/crypto/local\LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:24:    @Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode;

## FINDINGS

1. **Implemented default is trader-owned local ingestion, not the pending-only direction.** Checked-in `shared-market.mode` defaults to `OFF`; in OFF the trader starts Binance WebSocket ingestion, performs REST bootstrap/import, and persists to its local `crypto_ai.candle`.

2. **LIVE retains collector ownership.** In LIVE, trader WebSocket and REST ingestion throw/return early, candle reads route through the collector read pool targeting `crypto_ai_v2`, and local writes are rejected.

3. **OBSERVE is an architectural contradiction.** It is not collector-only:
   - Direct trader WebSocket and REST ingestion remain enabled because their guard is only `LIVE`.
   - It also creates a read-only collector pool and the shared consumer polls collector tables.
   - Bootstrap may generate `STARTUP` signals and call paper-trading processing in OBSERVE.
   - The direct-price pending resolver is OFF-only, so direct 1m OBSERVE events can remain `PENDING`, causing Replay to reject parity claims.

   The other report’s statement that OBSERVE “trades exactly as OFF” is too strong: code establishes that it can enter the analysis/paper-trading path, but a completed trade still depends on downstream strategy/risk/execution gates not traced here.

4. **Runtime close handling has the intended basic shape.** The runtime upsert assigns a close token only for an open→closed transition. The successful token-owning receipt publishes after input → protection → observer ordering, and async dispatcher work occurs after commit.

5. **Ordinary REST import violates the pending recovery/backfill contract.** Its `BACKFILL` duplicate-key clause can finalize an existing `closed=0` candle. It preserves provenance and adds no token, no analysis outcome, and no dispatch. Therefore a candle left open during shutdown can be closed by bootstrap before WebSocket startup and become ineligible for both normal token dispatch and reconciliation (which selects only `closed=0` rows).

6. **The implemented reconciliation slice is safer than ordinary REST import, but disabled and incomplete.** It is OFF-only and disabled by default. Its recovery SQL is update-only for existing open rows; gap repair is insert-if-absent; both produce `SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE` outcomes when successful. However:
   - candidate selection applies `LIMIT 50` before due/loss-evidence filtering;
   - only active-window candidates are selected, so a transient `PENDING` candidate that ages out is no longer selected and has no terminal age-out path;
   - no shared REST rate-limit/ban policy was found.

7. **Replay is not proven to reproduce the local live-admission boundary.** Replay loads any `closed=1` candles by time/identity, without filtering by runtime provenance, close token, reconciliation outcome, or historical-only status. It does correctly reject unresolved `PENDING` price evidence, but that is narrower than candle-admission parity.

8. **Fresh trader-only installation remains unresolved.** The applied Java migration `V103` explicitly fails unless `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event` exists, contradicting a fully collector-independent fresh bootstrap.

9. **Documentation conflicts with code and with itself.**
   - `AGENTS.md` calls local-candle runtime a pending design, while code defaults to OFF and both architecture documents say FIX-153 is implemented in source but unapproved.
   - `claude-docs` says direct ingestion is disabled in both LIVE and OBSERVE; code disables it only in LIVE.
   - `codex-docs` accurately describes ordinary REST finalization, but its adjacent “pending reconciliation” narrative is stale relative to the implemented, disabled reconciliation slice.
   - `CandleRepository` claims REST backfill “inserts missing identities only,” contradicted by its duplicate-key update SQL.

## OBJECTIONS

- Current default source configuration realizes much of the pending direction, but does not meet its release contract.
- Bootstrap REST can silently consume a runtime close without durable admission/outcome evidence.
- Disabled reconciliation cannot supply the required automatic recovery; its current retry/window behavior does not guarantee terminal resolution.
- OBSERVE simultaneously permits direct ingestion and collector connectivity, contrary to the required separation.
- No code evidence establishes shared Binance 429/418, `Retry-After`, or request-budget handling across bootstrap, reconciliation, and clock operations.
- Replay parity for provenance/outcome-based local candle admission is unproven.
- A collector-free fresh Flyway bootstrap is blocked by V103.

## UNKNOWNS

- Effective production artifact, mode, environment overrides, database/Flyway history, and runtime behavior were not accessed.
- The duplicate top-level `local-candle` YAML mapping is present, but whether this exact Spring Boot/SnakeYAML stack rejects it was not executed and remains unproven.
- Downstream OBSERVE strategy/risk/execution gates may prevent an individual startup signal from creating a paper trade.
- Java 21 build, MySQL integration, crash/restart, concurrency, and Replay parity gates were not run.
- External infrastructure may impose Binance request limits not represented in this repository.
